========================================
FILE: .github\workflows\main.yml

name: Build Android APK
on:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Set up JDK 21
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          java-version: '21'
          distribution: 'temurin'
      - name: Generate Debug Keystore
        run: |
          keytool -genkey -v -keystore debug.keystore -storepass android -alias androiddebugkey -keypass android -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 -dname "CN=Android Debug,O=Android,C=US"
          if [ -d "clien-volamidle-V5.2.2" ]; then cp debug.keystore clien-volamidle-V5.2.2/debug.keystore; fi
      - name: Setup Gradle
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v4
        with:
          gradle-version: '9.3.1'
      - name: Build Debug APK
        run: gradle assembleDebug --no-daemon
      - name: Upload APK
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: app-debug
          path: "**/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk"

                android:value="24/7 background game idle manager and partial wakelock" />
        </service>

             chính ta tạo) giao được Intent này. -->

            android:name="androidx.profileinstaller.ProfileInstallReceiver"
            tools:ignore="MissingClass"
            tools:node="remove" />
    </application>

========================================
FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\KeepAliveService.kt
========================================

package com.example

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.wifi.WifiManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.IBinder
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.PowerManager
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat

class KeepAliveService : Service() {

    private var wakeLock: PowerManager.WakeLock? = null
    private var wifiLock: WifiManager.WifiLock? = null
    private val refreshHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private var lockRefreshRunnable: Runnable? = null

    companion object {
        const val CHANNEL_ID = "volam_idle_service_channel"
        const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1001
        const val ACTION_START = "com.example.action.START_KEEP_ALIVE"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "com.example.action.STOP_KEEP_ALIVE"
        private const val WAKELOCK_DURATION_MS = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000L
        private const val LOCK_RENEW_INTERVAL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000L

        fun start(context: Context) {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(context, KeepAliveService::class.java).apply {
                    action = ACTION_START
                }
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                    context.startForegroundService(intent)
                } else {
                    context.startService(intent)
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }

        fun stop(context: Context) {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(context, KeepAliveService::class.java).apply {
                    action = ACTION_STOP
                }
                context.startService(intent)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        createNotificationChannel()
        acquireWakeLock()
        acquireWifiLock()
        scheduleLockRefresh()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        if (intent?.action == ACTION_STOP) {
            cancelLockRefresh()
            releaseWifiLock()
            releaseWakeLock()
            stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
            stopSelf()
            return START_NOT_STICKY
        }

        acquireWakeLock()
        acquireWifiLock()
        scheduleLockRefresh()

        val notification = buildNotification("Đang duy trì kết nối 24/7 (Chống ngắt & tiết kiệm pin)")
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            startForeground(
                NOTIFICATION_ID,
                notification,
                android.content.pm.ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_SPECIAL_USE
            )
        } else {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
        }

        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun scheduleLockRefresh() {
        if (lockRefreshRunnable != null) return
        val r = object : Runnable {
            override fun run() {
                acquireWakeLock(forceRenew = true)
                acquireWifiLock()
                refreshHandler.postDelayed(this, LOCK_RENEW_INTERVAL_MS)
            }
        }
        lockRefreshRunnable = r
        refreshHandler.postDelayed(r, LOCK_RENEW_INTERVAL_MS)
    }

    private fun cancelLockRefresh() {
        lockRefreshRunnable?.let { refreshHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        lockRefreshRunnable = null
    }

    private fun acquireWakeLock(forceRenew: Boolean = false) {
        try {
            if (wakeLock == null) {
                val powerManager = getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE) as? PowerManager
                wakeLock = powerManager?.newWakeLock(
                    PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK,
                    "VoLamIdle::KeepAliveWakeLock"
                )?.apply {
                    setReferenceCounted(false)
                }
            }
            if (forceRenew || wakeLock?.isHeld == false) {
                wakeLock?.acquire(WAKELOCK_DURATION_MS)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun acquireWifiLock() {
        if (wifiLock == null || wifiLock?.isHeld == false) {
            try {
                val wifiManager = applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as? WifiManager
                val lockMode = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                    WifiManager.WIFI_MODE_FULL_LOW_LATENCY
                } else {
                    WifiManager.WIFI_MODE_FULL_HIGH_PERF
                }
                wifiLock = wifiManager?.createWifiLock(lockMode, "VoLamIdle::LowLatencyWifiLock")?.apply {
                    setReferenceCounted(false)
                    acquire()
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    private fun releaseWifiLock() {
        try {
            if (wifiLock?.isHeld == true) {
                wifiLock?.release()
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        wifiLock = null
    }

    private fun releaseWakeLock() {
        try {
            if (wakeLock?.isHeld == true) {
                wakeLock?.release()
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        wakeLock = null
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(
                CHANNEL_ID,
                "Võ Lâm Idle Service",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
            ).apply {
                description = "Duy trì kết nối mạng cho các tài khoản Võ Lâm Idle"
                setShowBadge(false)
            }
            val manager = getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
            manager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    private fun buildNotification(statusText: String): Notification {
        val launchIntent = packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(packageName)
        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            0,
            launchIntent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        return NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle("Võ Lâm Idle Manager")
            .setContentText(statusText)
            .setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic_launcher)
            .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
            .build()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        cancelLockRefresh()
        releaseWifiLock()
        releaseWakeLock()
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null
}

package com.example

import android.os.Bundle

import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.content.res.Configuration
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat

import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalConfiguration
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.example.model.ViewLayoutMode
import com.example.sleep.SleepWakeController
import com.example.ui.MainViewModel
import com.example.ui.components.AccountDialog
import com.example.ui.components.AutoConfigDialog
import com.example.ui.components.ControlSheet
import com.example.ui.components.hasDownloadTraffic
import com.example.ui.components.GameWebViewContainer
import com.example.ui.components.OledBlackScreen
import com.example.ui.components.SidebarView
import com.example.ui.components.SleepWakeSetupDialog
import com.example.ui.theme.MyApplicationTheme
import com.example.update.AppVisibility
import com.example.update.UpdateUiState
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import androidx.compose.foundation.border

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {

    private val viewModel: MainViewModel by viewModels()

    private val screenWakeReceiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
        override fun onReceive(context: Context?, intent: Intent?) {
            if (!viewModel.isSleepMode.value) return
            when (intent?.action) {
                Intent.ACTION_SCREEN_ON, Intent.ACTION_USER_PRESENT -> {
                    applySleepBrightness(true)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun applySleepBrightness(low: Boolean) {
        val layoutParams = window.attributes
        val target = if (low) 0.01f else WindowManager.LayoutParams.BRIGHTNESS_OVERRIDE_NONE
        if (layoutParams.screenBrightness != target) {
            layoutParams.screenBrightness = target
            window.attributes = layoutParams
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()

        // Keep screen on while farming 24/7
        window.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)

        val wakeFilter = IntentFilter().apply {
            addAction(Intent.ACTION_SCREEN_ON)
            addAction(Intent.ACTION_USER_PRESENT)
        }
        runCatching {
            ContextCompat.registerReceiver(
                this,
                screenWakeReceiver,
                wakeFilter,
                ContextCompat.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED
            )
        }

        setContent {
            MyApplicationTheme(darkTheme = true) {
                MainScreen(
                    viewModel = viewModel,
                    onSetWindowBrightnessLow = { low ->
                        applySleepBrightness(low)
                    }
                )
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        // AppOp "Cài đặt ứng dụng không rõ nguồn gốc" không có callback kết quả: kiểm lại
        // ở đây. KHÔNG tự cài — chỉ đưa UI về trạng thái bấm được "Cài đặt".
        viewModel.onActivityResumed()
        viewModel.tabManager.onAppForegrounded()
        if (viewModel.isSleepMode.value) {
            applySleepBrightness(!viewModel.isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen.value)
        }
    }

    override fun onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus: Boolean) {
        super.onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus)
        if (hasFocus && viewModel.isSleepMode.value) {
            applySleepBrightness(!viewModel.isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen.value)
        }
    }

    override fun onStart() {
        super.onStart()
        // PackageInstaller chỉ được commit từ Activity đang STARTED (Android 10+ chặn BAL),
        // và STATUS_PENDING_USER_ACTION chỉ được startActivity khi app visible.
        AppVisibility.onActivityStarted()
    }

    override fun onPause() {
        super.onPause()
        viewModel.tabManager.onAppBackgrounded()
    }

    override fun onStop() {
        super.onStop()
        AppVisibility.onActivityStopped()
        viewModel.tabManager.onAppBackgrounded()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        runCatching { unregisterReceiver(screenWakeReceiver) }
        if (isFinishing && !isChangingConfigurations && viewModel.isSleepMode.value) {
            viewModel.setSleepMode(false)
        }
        super.onDestroy()
    }
}

@Composable
fun MainScreen(
    viewModel: MainViewModel,
    onSetWindowBrightnessLow: (Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
    val isPortrait = configuration.orientation == Configuration.ORIENTATION_PORTRAIT

    val isMasterAutoRunning by viewModel.isMasterAutoRunning.collectAsState()
    val characterStatsList by viewModel.characterStatsList.collectAsState()
    val selectedTabIndex by viewModel.selectedTabIndex.collectAsState()
    val networkData by viewModel.networkData.collectAsState()
    // Ô ⋯ đổi màu theo lưu lượng tải về (§8). Truyền Boolean thay vì cả NetworkDataInfo để
    // container không phải recompose mỗi 2 giây khi số byte đổi mà tốc độ vẫn bằng 0.
    val isDownloading = hasDownloadTraffic(networkData.downloadSpeedFormatted)
    val fps by viewModel.fps.collectAsState()
    val isSleepMode by viewModel.isSleepMode.collectAsState()
    val hasDndPolicyAccess by viewModel.hasDndPolicyAccess.collectAsState()
    val isNotificationWakePaused by viewModel.isNotificationWakePaused.collectAsState()
    val isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen by viewModel.isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen.collectAsState()
    val isSidebarVisible by viewModel.isSidebarVisible.collectAsState()
    val layoutMode by viewModel.layoutMode.collectAsState()
    val editingAccount by viewModel.editingAccount.collectAsState()
    val isBlackScreenSynced by viewModel.isBlackScreenSynced.collectAsState()
    val isHideMapSynced by viewModel.isHideMapSynced.collectAsState()
    val autoConfigs by viewModel.autoConfigs.collectAsState()
    val autoDialogTabIndex by viewModel.autoDialogTabIndex.collectAsState()
    val tabCount by viewModel.tabCount.collectAsState()
    val tabCountWarnShown by viewModel.tabCountWarnShown.collectAsState()

    // Bảng điều khiển nhanh: mọi control từng nằm ở header 44dp + mini-header 28dp đều ở đây.
    val isControlSheetOpen by viewModel.isControlSheetOpen.collectAsState()
    val controlSheetTabIndex by viewModel.controlSheetTabIndex.collectAsState()
    val sheetTabStats = characterStatsList.getOrElse(controlSheetTabIndex) {
        com.example.model.CharacterStats(tabIndex = controlSheetTabIndex)
    }

    // ===== Tự động cập nhật: banner nhắc không chặn (32dp) =====
    // Chỉ nhắc khi CÓ bản mới và tối đa 1 lần / versionCode. Ẩn khi Treo máy (màn hình cố ý
    // đen 0.01), không dialog, không cướp focus, tự ẩn sau 8s.
    val updateState by viewModel.updateState.collectAsState()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var updateBanner by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    // Hộp thoại nhắc cập nhật khi MỞ APP (không cần mở bảng điều khiển). Khác banner 32dp:
    // chỉ hiện một lần / versionCode, không hiện khi Treo máy, và chỉ có hai lựa chọn.
    val foregroundTick by viewModel.foregroundTick.collectAsState()
    var updateNoticeVersion by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    LaunchedEffect(updateState, isSleepMode, updateNoticeVersion) {
        // Hộp thoại nhắc đang mở thì không vẽ thêm banner 32dp phía sau nó. Đóng hộp thoại
        // ("Để sau") sẽ chạy lại hiệu ứng này và banner có thể hiện — hộp thoại thì không lặp lại.
        if (isSleepMode || updateNoticeVersion != null) {
            updateBanner = null
            return@LaunchedEffect
        }
        val state = updateState
        if (state is UpdateUiState.Available && viewModel.shouldShowUpdateBanner()) {
            viewModel.markUpdateBannerShown()
            updateBanner = context.getString(R.string.update_banner_available, state.versionName)
        }
    }

    // Thông điệp "đã cập nhật lên X" của process trước (kết quả cài tới muộn).
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        viewModel.consumeLaunchMessage()?.let { message -> updateBanner = message }
    }

    // Xét nhắc cập nhật ở BA thời điểm: composition đầu (cold start đọc release từ cache),
    // mỗi lần Activity resume (foregroundTick), và khi trạng thái chuyển sang Available do
    // lần kiểm tra 3 giây sau khởi động. `takeUpdateNotice()` tự đánh dấu đã nhắc nên ba
    // nguồn này không thể hiện hai lần cho cùng một versionCode.
    // Treo máy thì bỏ qua và KHÔNG đánh dấu: đèn hình đang cố ý tắt.
    LaunchedEffect(foregroundTick, updateState is UpdateUiState.Available, isSleepMode) {
        if (isSleepMode || updateNoticeVersion != null) return@LaunchedEffect
        viewModel.takeUpdateNotice()?.let { version -> updateNoticeVersion = version }
    }

    // Hẹn giờ tách riêng, key theo CHÍNH nội dung banner: nếu một hiệu ứng khác đổi banner
    // thì đồng hồ cũ bị huỷ và đặt lại — không có đường nào để banner kẹt vĩnh viễn.
    LaunchedEffect(updateBanner) {
        if (updateBanner != null) {
            delay(8_000)
            updateBanner = null
        }
    }

    // Handle brightness adjustments when Sleep Mode toggles
    LaunchedEffect(isSleepMode, isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen) {
        onSetWindowBrightnessLow(isSleepMode && !isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen)
    }

    // Back button handling
    BackHandler(enabled = isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen || isSleepMode || editingAccount != null || autoDialogTabIndex != null || isControlSheetOpen || (isPortrait && isSidebarVisible)) {
        when {
            isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen -> viewModel.dismissSleepWakeSetupDialog()
            isSleepMode -> viewModel.setSleepMode(false)
            isControlSheetOpen -> viewModel.dismissControlSheet()
            editingAccount != null -> viewModel.dismissAccountDialog()
            autoDialogTabIndex != null -> viewModel.dismissAutoConfigDialog()
            isPortrait && isSidebarVisible -> viewModel.toggleSidebar()
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color(0xFF090D14)),
        // Không để Scaffold tự chừa inset: dải 32dp ở đáy phải là thứ duy nhất báo
        // chiều cao chrome, và nó tự lo phần nav inset của chính nó.
        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0, 0, 0, 0)
    ) { innerPadding ->
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(innerPadding)
        ) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .windowInsetsPadding(
                        WindowInsets.safeDrawing.only(
                            WindowInsetsSides.Top + WindowInsetsSides.Horizontal
                        )
                    )
            ) {
                // Main Workspace: Tối ưu cho cả màn hình dọc (RedMagic 8 Pro, Vivo X100 Ultra) và màn ngang PC
                if (isPortrait) {
                    // Màn hình dọc: GameWebView chiếm 100% toàn màn hình; Bảng điều khiển là Drawer trượt đè lên
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .weight(1f)
                    ) {
                        GameWebViewContainer(
                            tabManager = viewModel.tabManager,
                            tabCount = tabCount,
                            characterStatsList = characterStatsList,
                            selectedTabIndex = selectedTabIndex,
                            layoutMode = layoutMode,
                            isDownloading = isDownloading,
                            onSelectTab = { viewModel.selectTab(it) },
                            onOpenControlSheet = { viewModel.openControlSheet(it) },
                            onOpenSidebar = {
                                viewModel.tabManager.markUiInteraction()
                                viewModel.toggleSidebar()
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                        )

                        if (isSidebarVisible) {
                            // Lớp phủ mờ (Scrim) - chạm vào đóng bảng
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxSize()
                                    .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.65f))
                                    .clickable { viewModel.toggleSidebar() }
                            )

                            // Bảng điều khiển (Sidebar) hiển thị đè lên bên trái với bề rộng 320dp
                            SidebarView(
                                isMasterAutoRunning = isMasterAutoRunning,
                                tabCount = tabCount,
                                characterStatsList = characterStatsList,
                                selectedTabIndex = selectedTabIndex,
                                onSelectTab = { viewModel.selectTab(it) },
                                onLoginAll = { viewModel.onMasterLoginAllClicked() },
                                onStopAll = { viewModel.onMasterStopAllClicked() },
                                onEditAccount = { viewModel.startEditingAccount(it) },
                                onToggleSidebar = { viewModel.toggleSidebar() },
                                getAccount = { viewModel.prefs.getAccount(it) },
                                isBlackScreenSynced = isBlackScreenSynced,
                                onSyncBlackScreen = { viewModel.syncBlackScreen(it) },
                                isHideMapSynced = isHideMapSynced,
                                onSyncHideMap = { viewModel.syncHideMap(it) },
                                onSyncSellExcess = { viewModel.syncSellExcessItems() },
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxHeight()
                                    .width(320.dp)
                                    .align(Alignment.CenterStart)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    // Màn hình ngang (Tablet / PC): Bảng điều khiển và WebViews nằm cạnh nhau (Row)
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .weight(1f)
                    ) {
                        if (isSidebarVisible) {
                            SidebarView(
                                isMasterAutoRunning = isMasterAutoRunning,
                                tabCount = tabCount,
                                characterStatsList = characterStatsList,
                                selectedTabIndex = selectedTabIndex,
                                onSelectTab = { viewModel.selectTab(it) },
                                onLoginAll = { viewModel.onMasterLoginAllClicked() },
                                onStopAll = { viewModel.onMasterStopAllClicked() },
                                onEditAccount = { viewModel.startEditingAccount(it) },
                                onToggleSidebar = { viewModel.toggleSidebar() },
                                getAccount = { viewModel.prefs.getAccount(it) },
                                isBlackScreenSynced = isBlackScreenSynced,
                                onSyncBlackScreen = { viewModel.syncBlackScreen(it) },
                                isHideMapSynced = isHideMapSynced,
                                onSyncHideMap = { viewModel.syncHideMap(it) },
                                onSyncSellExcess = { viewModel.syncSellExcessItems() },
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .width(265.dp)
                                    .windowInsetsPadding(
                                        WindowInsets.navigationBars.only(WindowInsetsSides.Bottom)
                                    )
                            )
                        }

                        GameWebViewContainer(
                            tabManager = viewModel.tabManager,
                            tabCount = tabCount,
                            characterStatsList = characterStatsList,
                            selectedTabIndex = selectedTabIndex,
                            layoutMode = layoutMode,
                            isDownloading = isDownloading,
                            onSelectTab = { viewModel.selectTab(it) },
                            onOpenControlSheet = { viewModel.openControlSheet(it) },
                            onOpenSidebar = {
                                viewModel.tabManager.markUiInteraction()
                                viewModel.toggleSidebar()
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .fillMaxHeight()
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // Banner cập nhật: overlay 32dp ở ĐỈNH, không đẩy WebView xuống (không reflow HUD).
            updateBanner?.takeIf { !isSleepMode }?.let { text ->
                val bannerShape = RoundedCornerShape(6.dp)
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .align(Alignment.TopCenter)
                        .padding(start = 8.dp, end = 8.dp, top = 6.dp)
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .height(32.dp)
                        .clip(bannerShape)
                        .background(Color(0xFF0F172A))
                        .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF38BDF8), bannerShape)
                        .clickable {
                            viewModel.tabManager.markUiInteraction()
                            updateBanner = null
                            viewModel.openControlSheet(selectedTabIndex)
                        }
                        .padding(horizontal = 10.dp)
                        .testTag("update_banner"),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = text,
                        color = Color(0xFFE2E8F0),
                        fontSize = 10.5.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }
            }

            // Bảng điều khiển nhanh (overlay — KHÔNG đẩy WebView xuống).
            // Đặt TRƯỚC OledBlackScreen để chế độ treo máy che lên trên; mở khoá quay lại sheet.
            ControlSheet(
                isOpen = isControlSheetOpen,
                tabIndex = controlSheetTabIndex,
                tabLabel = sheetTabStats.characterName,
                layoutMode = layoutMode,
                fps = fps,
                networkData = networkData,
                onOpenTab = {
                    viewModel.selectTab(controlSheetTabIndex)
                    viewModel.tabManager.openSingleTab(controlSheetTabIndex)
                },
                onReloadTab = { viewModel.reloadTab(controlSheetTabIndex) },
                onStopTab = { viewModel.stopSingleTab(controlSheetTabIndex) },
                onChangeLayoutMode = { viewModel.setLayoutMode(it) },
                onToggleFps = { viewModel.toggleFps() },
                onActivateSleepMode = { viewModel.onSleepModeRequested() },
                onOpenSidebar = {
                    // Drawer nằm dưới sheet trong cây view: đóng sheet trước rồi mới mở drawer.
                    viewModel.dismissControlSheet()
                    viewModel.toggleSidebar()
                },
                onResetNetworkStats = { viewModel.resetNetworkCounters() },
                onSyncAutoMode = { viewModel.syncAutoMode(it) },
                isAutoPilotOn = autoConfigs.getOrNull(controlSheetTabIndex)?.on == true,
                onOpenAutoConfig = {
                    // Drawer nằm dưới sheet: đóng sheet trước để hộp thoại không bị che.
                    viewModel.dismissControlSheet()
                    viewModel.openAutoConfigDialog(controlSheetTabIndex)
                },
                tabCount = tabCount,
                maxTabCount = viewModel.maxTabCount,
                isLowRam = viewModel.isLowRam,
                tabCountWarnShown = tabCountWarnShown,
                hasBusyTabsInRange = { from, toExclusive ->
                    viewModel.hasBusyTabsInRange(from, toExclusive)
                },
                onSetTabCount = { viewModel.setTabCount(it) },
                onMarkTabCountWarned = { viewModel.markTabCountWarned() },
                appVersionName = viewModel.appVersionName,
                updateState = updateState,
                onCheckUpdate = { viewModel.checkForUpdate() },
                onStartDownload = { viewModel.startUpdateDownload() },
                onInstallUpdate = { viewModel.installUpdate() },
                onOpenReleases = {
                    val open = android.content.Intent(
                        android.content.Intent.ACTION_VIEW,
                        android.net.Uri.parse(viewModel.releasesPageUrl())
                    )
                    runCatching { context.startActivity(open) }
                },
                onDismiss = { viewModel.dismissControlSheet() },
                onInteract = { viewModel.tabManager.markUiInteraction() },
                hasDndPolicyAccess = hasDndPolicyAccess,
                onOpenSleepWakeSetup = { viewModel.openSleepWakeSetupDialog() },
                isHideMap = isHideMapSynced,
                onToggleHideMap = { viewModel.syncHideMap(!isHideMapSynced) }
            )

            // MODULE 4: OLED Black Screen Overlay (Double tap to unlock)
            if (isSleepMode) {
                OledBlackScreen(
                    onUnlock = { viewModel.setSleepMode(false) },
                    isNotificationWakePaused = isNotificationWakePaused,
                    hasDndPolicyAccess = hasDndPolicyAccess,
                    onOpenWakeSetup = { viewModel.openSleepWakeSetupDialog() }
                )
            }

            if (isSleepWakeSetupDialogOpen) {
                SleepWakeSetupDialog(
                    hasDndPolicyAccess = hasDndPolicyAccess,
                    isAlreadyInSleepMode = isSleepMode,
                    onOpenDndSettings = {
                        runCatching {
                            context.startActivity(SleepWakeController.dndPolicySettingsIntent())
                        }
                    },
                    onOpenDisplaySettings = {
                        runCatching {
                            context.startActivity(SleepWakeController.displaySettingsIntent())
                        }
                    },
                    onConfirmSleepMode = { viewModel.confirmEnterSleepModeFromSetup() },
                    onDismiss = { viewModel.dismissSleepWakeSetupDialog() }
                )
            }

            // Edit Account Dialog
            editingAccount?.let { account ->
                AccountDialog(
                    accountConfig = account,
                    onSave = { updated -> viewModel.saveEditingAccount(updated) },
                    onDelete = { deleted -> viewModel.deleteAccount(deleted.tabIndex) },
                    onDismiss = { viewModel.dismissAccountDialog() }
                )
            }

            // Bảng 🤖 Tự động (cấu hình và hoạt động độc lập cho từng tab)
            autoDialogTabIndex?.let { dialogTab ->
                val safeTab = dialogTab.coerceIn(0, (tabCount - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
                val tabCharName = characterStatsList.getOrNull(safeTab)?.characterName.orEmpty()
                val tabSects = characterStatsList.map { it.sect }
                AutoConfigDialog(
                    tabIndex = safeTab,
                    tabCount = tabCount,
                    configs = autoConfigs,
                    characterName = tabCharName,
                    characterSects = tabSects,
                    onSelectTab = { newTab -> viewModel.switchAutoDialogTab(newTab) },
                    onSave = { tIdx, updated -> viewModel.saveAutoConfig(tIdx, updated) },
                    onSaveAll = { updated -> viewModel.saveAllAutoConfig(updated) },
                    onResetCounters = { tIdx -> viewModel.resetAutoDailyCounters(tIdx) },
                    onDismiss = { viewModel.dismissAutoConfigDialog() }
                )
            }

            // Nhắc cập nhật khi mở app: đúng hai lựa chọn. "Cập nhật" đi vào luồng tải/cài
            // hiện có trong bảng điều khiển; "Để sau" chỉ đóng hộp thoại — banner 32dp có thể
            // nhắc lại, nhưng hộp thoại này thì không (cờ update_dialog_shown_for theo versionCode).
            updateNoticeVersion?.let { version ->
                AlertDialog(
                    onDismissRequest = { updateNoticeVersion = null },
                    containerColor = Color(0xFF1E293B),
                    title = {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.update_notice_title, version),
                            color = Color(0xFFE2E8F0),
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    },
                    text = {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(
                                R.string.update_notice_body,
                                viewModel.appVersionName
                            ),
                            color = Color(0xFFCBD5E1),
                            fontSize = 11.sp
                        )
                    },
                    confirmButton = {
                        TextButton(
                            onClick = {
                                updateNoticeVersion = null
                                viewModel.onUpdateNoticeAccepted()
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("update_notice_update")
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.update_notice_update),
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                        }
                    },
                    dismissButton = {
                        TextButton(
                            onClick = { updateNoticeVersion = null },
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("update_notice_later")
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.update_notice_later),
                                fontSize = 11.sp
                            )
                        }
                    }
                )
            }
        }
    }
}


========================================
FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\VolamIdleApp.kt
========================================

package com.example

import android.app.Application
import android.content.ComponentCallbacks2
import com.example.data.PreferencesManager
import com.example.sleep.SleepWakeController
import com.example.update.UpdateRepository
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlin.random.Random

class VolamIdleApp : Application() {

    val processScope: CoroutineScope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default)

    lateinit var updateRepository: UpdateRepository
        private set

    @Volatile
    var onMemoryTrimRequested: ((Int) -> Unit)? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        updateRepository = UpdateRepository(this, processScope)
        if (!isUnitTestRuntime()) {
            SleepWakeController(this, PreferencesManager(this)).restoreIfOrphaned()
            startUpdateLoop()
        }
    }

    override fun onTrimMemory(level: Int) {
        super.onTrimMemory(level)
        if (level >= ComponentCallbacks2.TRIM_MEMORY_RUNNING_LOW) {
            onMemoryTrimRequested?.invoke(level)
        }
    }

    override fun onLowMemory() {
        super.onLowMemory()
        onMemoryTrimRequested?.invoke(ComponentCallbacks2.TRIM_MEMORY_COMPLETE)
    }

    private fun isUnitTestRuntime(): Boolean =
        android.os.Build.FINGERPRINT.contains("robolectric", ignoreCase = true)

    private fun startUpdateLoop() {
        processScope.launch {
            delay(FIRST_CHECK_DELAY_MS)
            runCatching { updateRepository.checkForUpdate(manual = false) }

            while (true) {
                val jitter = Random.nextLong(-AUTO_CHECK_JITTER_MS, AUTO_CHECK_JITTER_MS + 1)
                delay(UpdateRepository.AUTO_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS + jitter)
                runCatching { updateRepository.checkForUpdate(manual = false) }
            }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val FIRST_CHECK_DELAY_MS = 3_000L
        private const val AUTO_CHECK_JITTER_MS = UpdateRepository.AUTO_CHECK_JITTER_MS
    }
}


========================================
FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\data\PreferencesManager.kt
========================================

package com.example.data

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import com.example.model.AccountConfig

class PreferencesManager(context: Context) {

    private val prefs: SharedPreferences =
        context.getSharedPreferences("volam_idle_manager_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    companion object {
        private const val KEY_CLUSTER = "pref_cluster"
        private const val KEY_FPS = "pref_fps"
        private const val KEY_RX_OFFSET = "pref_rx_offset"
        private const val KEY_TX_OFFSET = "pref_tx_offset"
        private const val KEY_LEGACY_USER_PREFIX = "pref_user_tab_"
        private const val KEY_LEGACY_PASS_PREFIX = "pref_pass_tab_"
        private const val KEY_LEGACY_AUTO_PREFIX = "pref_auto_tab_"
        private const val KEY_LEGACY_URL_PREFIX = "pref_url_tab_"

        private const val KEY_AUTO_CFG = "pref_auto_cfg"
        private const val KEY_AUTO_CFG_TAB_PREFIX = "pref_auto_cfg_tab_"

        private const val KEY_TAB_COUNT = "pref_tab_count"
        private const val KEY_TAB_WARN_SHOWN = "pref_tab_warn_shown"
        private const val KEY_HIDE_MAP = "pref_hide_map"

        private const val KEY_UPDATE_LAST_ATTEMPT = "update_last_check_attempt"
        private const val KEY_UPDATE_LAST_MANUAL = "update_last_manual_check"
        private const val KEY_UPDATE_ETAG = "update_etag"
        private const val KEY_UPDATE_BLOCKED_UNTIL = "update_blocked_until"
        private const val KEY_UPDATE_CACHED_RELEASE = "update_cached_release"
        private const val KEY_UPDATE_BANNER_SHOWN_FOR = "update_banner_shown_for"
        private const val KEY_UPDATE_DIALOG_SHOWN_FOR = "update_dialog_shown_for"
        private const val KEY_UPDATE_INSTALL_RESULT = "update_install_result"
        private const val KEY_UPDATE_PENDING_ACTION = "pending_action"

        private const val KEY_DND_OVERRIDE_ACTIVE = "sleep_dnd_override_active"
        private const val KEY_DND_PREV_FILTER = "sleep_dnd_prev_filter"
        private const val KEY_DND_PREV_CATEGORIES = "sleep_dnd_prev_categories"
        private const val KEY_DND_PREV_CALL_SENDERS = "sleep_dnd_prev_call_senders"
        private const val KEY_DND_PREV_MSG_SENDERS = "sleep_dnd_prev_msg_senders"
        private const val KEY_DND_PREV_VISUAL_EFFECTS = "sleep_dnd_prev_visual_effects"
        private const val KEY_DND_PREV_CONV_SENDERS = "sleep_dnd_prev_conv_senders"
        private const val KEY_SLEEP_WAKE_PROMPT_SHOWN = "sleep_wake_prompt_shown"

        private const val GAME_HOST = "volamidle.pages.dev"

        fun isTrustedGameUrl(url: String): Boolean {
            val trimmed = url.trim()
            if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return true
            val uri = try {
                android.net.Uri.parse(trimmed)
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                return false
            }
            if (uri.scheme?.lowercase() != "https") return false
            val host = uri.host?.lowercase() ?: return false
            return host == GAME_HOST || host.endsWith(".$GAME_HOST")
        }
    }

    fun getAutoConfig(): String = prefs.getString(KEY_AUTO_CFG, "") ?: ""

    fun setAutoConfig(json: String) {
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_AUTO_CFG, json).commit()
    }

    fun getAutoConfig(tabIndex: Int): String {
        val perTab = prefs.getString("$KEY_AUTO_CFG_TAB_PREFIX$tabIndex", null)
        return perTab ?: ""
    }

    fun setAutoConfig(tabIndex: Int, json: String) {
        prefs.edit().putString("$KEY_AUTO_CFG_TAB_PREFIX$tabIndex", json).commit()
    }

    private fun getClusterKey(cluster: String): String {
        return cluster.replace(" ", "_").lowercase()
    }

    fun getCluster(): String {
        return prefs.getString(KEY_CLUSTER, "Cụm 1") ?: "Cụm 1"
    }

    fun setCluster(cluster: String) {
        prefs.edit().putString(KEY_CLUSTER, cluster).commit()
    }

    fun getClusterUrl(cluster: String): String {
        return when (cluster) {
            "Cụm 2" -> "https://volamidle.pages.dev"
            "Cụm 3" -> "https://volamidle.pages.dev"
            else -> "https://volamidle.pages.dev"
        }
    }

    fun getFps(): Int {
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_FPS, 30)
    }

        prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_FPS, fps).apply()
    }

    }

    fun getAccount(tabIndex: Int, cluster: String = getCluster()): AccountConfig {
        val cKey = getClusterKey(cluster)
        val clusterNum = when (cluster) {
            "Cụm 2" -> "2"
            "Cụm 3" -> "3"
            else -> "1"
        }
        val clusterUserKey = "user_cluster_${cKey}_tab_$tabIndex"
        val clusterPassKey = "pass_cluster_${cKey}_tab_$tabIndex"
        val clusterAutoKey = "auto_cluster_${cKey}_tab_$tabIndex"
        val clusterUrlKey = "url_cluster_${cKey}_tab_$tabIndex"

        var user = prefs.getString(clusterUserKey, "") ?: ""
        var pass = prefs.getString(clusterPassKey, "") ?: ""
        var auto = prefs.getBoolean(clusterAutoKey, true)
        var customUrl = prefs.getString(clusterUrlKey, "") ?: ""

        if (user.isBlank()) {
            val numUser = prefs.getString("user_cluster_${clusterNum}_tab_$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
            if (numUser.isNotBlank()) {
                user = numUser
                pass = prefs.getString("pass_cluster_${clusterNum}_tab_$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
                auto = prefs.getBoolean("auto_cluster_${clusterNum}_tab_$tabIndex", true)
                customUrl = prefs.getString("url_cluster_${clusterNum}_tab_$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
            }
        }

        if (user.isBlank()) {
            val directUser = prefs.getString("user_cluster_${cluster}_tab_$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
            if (directUser.isNotBlank()) {
                user = directUser
                pass = prefs.getString("pass_cluster_${cluster}_tab_$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
                auto = prefs.getBoolean("auto_cluster_${cluster}_tab_$tabIndex", true)
                customUrl = prefs.getString("url_cluster_${cluster}_tab_$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
            }
        }

        if (user.isBlank()) {
            val legacyUser = prefs.getString("$KEY_LEGACY_USER_PREFIX$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
            if (legacyUser.isNotBlank()) {
                user = legacyUser
                pass = prefs.getString("$KEY_LEGACY_PASS_PREFIX$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
                auto = prefs.getBoolean("$KEY_LEGACY_AUTO_PREFIX$tabIndex", true)
                customUrl = prefs.getString("$KEY_LEGACY_URL_PREFIX$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
            }
        }

        if (user.isBlank()) {
            val bareUser = prefs.getString("user_tab_$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
            if (bareUser.isNotBlank()) {
                user = bareUser
                pass = prefs.getString("pass_tab_$tabIndex", "") ?: ""
            }
        }

        return AccountConfig(tabIndex, user.trim(), pass.trim(), auto, customUrl.trim())
    }

    fun saveAccount(config: AccountConfig, cluster: String = getCluster()) {
        val cKey = getClusterKey(cluster)
        val clusterNum = when (cluster) {
            "Cụm 2" -> "2"
            "Cụm 3" -> "3"
            else -> "1"
        }
        val cleanUser = config.username.trim()
        val cleanPass = config.password.trim()
        val cleanUrl = config.customUrl.trim()

        prefs.edit()
            .putString("user_cluster_${cKey}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanUser)
            .putString("pass_cluster_${cKey}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanPass)
            .putBoolean("auto_cluster_${cKey}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", config.autoLoginEnabled)
            .putString("url_cluster_${cKey}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanUrl)
            .putString("user_cluster_${clusterNum}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanUser)
            .putString("pass_cluster_${clusterNum}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanPass)
            .putBoolean("auto_cluster_${clusterNum}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", config.autoLoginEnabled)
            .putString("url_cluster_${clusterNum}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanUrl)
            .putString("user_cluster_${cluster}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanUser)
            .putString("pass_cluster_${cluster}_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanPass)
            .putString("$KEY_LEGACY_USER_PREFIX${config.tabIndex}", cleanUser)
            .putString("$KEY_LEGACY_PASS_PREFIX${config.tabIndex}", cleanPass)
            .putBoolean("$KEY_LEGACY_AUTO_PREFIX${config.tabIndex}", config.autoLoginEnabled)
            .putString("$KEY_LEGACY_URL_PREFIX${config.tabIndex}", cleanUrl)
            .putString("user_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanUser)
            .putString("pass_tab_${config.tabIndex}", cleanPass)
            .commit()
    }

    fun clearAccountEverywhere(tabIndex: Int) {
        val editor = prefs.edit()

        val clusterIds = listOf("1", "2", "3") +
            listOf("Cụm 1", "Cụm 2", "Cụm 3") +
            listOf("Cụm 1", "Cụm 2", "Cụm 3").map { getClusterKey(it) }
        for (id in clusterIds.distinct()) {
            editor.putString("user_cluster_${id}_tab_$tabIndex", "")
            editor.putString("pass_cluster_${id}_tab_$tabIndex", "")
            editor.putString("url_cluster_${id}_tab_$tabIndex", "")
            editor.putBoolean("auto_cluster_${id}_tab_$tabIndex", false)
        }

        editor.putString("$KEY_LEGACY_USER_PREFIX$tabIndex", "")
        editor.putString("$KEY_LEGACY_PASS_PREFIX$tabIndex", "")
        editor.putString("$KEY_LEGACY_URL_PREFIX$tabIndex", "")
        editor.putBoolean("$KEY_LEGACY_AUTO_PREFIX$tabIndex", false)
        editor.putString("user_tab_$tabIndex", "")
        editor.putString("pass_tab_$tabIndex", "")
        editor.putString("url_tab_$tabIndex", "")
        editor.putBoolean("auto_tab_$tabIndex", false)

        editor.commit()
    }

    fun getRxOffset(): Long = prefs.getLong(KEY_RX_OFFSET, 0L)

    data class SavedDndSnapshot(
        val interruptionFilter: Int,
        val priorityCategories: Int,
        val priorityCallSenders: Int,
        val priorityMessageSenders: Int,
        val suppressedVisualEffects: Int,
        val priorityConversationSenders: Int
    )

    fun isDndOverrideActive(): Boolean = prefs.getBoolean(KEY_DND_OVERRIDE_ACTIVE, false)

    fun saveDndSnapshot(snapshot: SavedDndSnapshot) {
        prefs.edit()
            .putBoolean(KEY_DND_OVERRIDE_ACTIVE, true)
            .putInt(KEY_DND_PREV_FILTER, snapshot.interruptionFilter)
            .putInt(KEY_DND_PREV_CATEGORIES, snapshot.priorityCategories)
            .putInt(KEY_DND_PREV_CALL_SENDERS, snapshot.priorityCallSenders)
            .putInt(KEY_DND_PREV_MSG_SENDERS, snapshot.priorityMessageSenders)
            .putInt(KEY_DND_PREV_VISUAL_EFFECTS, snapshot.suppressedVisualEffects)
            .putInt(KEY_DND_PREV_CONV_SENDERS, snapshot.priorityConversationSenders)
            .commit()
    }

    fun getDndSnapshot(): SavedDndSnapshot? {
        if (!isDndOverrideActive()) return null
        return SavedDndSnapshot(
            interruptionFilter = prefs.getInt(KEY_DND_PREV_FILTER, 1),
            priorityCategories = prefs.getInt(KEY_DND_PREV_CATEGORIES, 0),
            priorityCallSenders = prefs.getInt(KEY_DND_PREV_CALL_SENDERS, 0),
            priorityMessageSenders = prefs.getInt(KEY_DND_PREV_MSG_SENDERS, 0),
            suppressedVisualEffects = prefs.getInt(KEY_DND_PREV_VISUAL_EFFECTS, 0),
            priorityConversationSenders = prefs.getInt(KEY_DND_PREV_CONV_SENDERS, 0)
        )
    }

    fun clearDndSnapshot() {
        prefs.edit()
            .putBoolean(KEY_DND_OVERRIDE_ACTIVE, false)
            .remove(KEY_DND_PREV_FILTER)
            .remove(KEY_DND_PREV_CATEGORIES)
            .remove(KEY_DND_PREV_CALL_SENDERS)
            .remove(KEY_DND_PREV_MSG_SENDERS)
            .remove(KEY_DND_PREV_VISUAL_EFFECTS)
            .remove(KEY_DND_PREV_CONV_SENDERS)
            .commit()
    }

    fun isSleepWakePromptShown(): Boolean = prefs.getBoolean(KEY_SLEEP_WAKE_PROMPT_SHOWN, false)

    fun setSleepWakePromptShown(shown: Boolean) {
        prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_SLEEP_WAKE_PROMPT_SHOWN, shown).apply()
    }
}

FILE: app\src\main\java\com\example\model\TabLimits.kt
