  function handleCanvasSpyText(ctx, text, rawX, rawY) {
    try {
      if (!ctx || !ctx.canvas) return;
      var cv = ctx.canvas;
      if (!cv || cv.width < 120 || cv.height < 120) return;
      var idCls = ((cv.id || '') + ' ' + (typeof cv.className === 'string' ? cv.className : '')).toLowerCase();
      if (idCls.indexOf('minimap') >= 0 || idCls.indexOf('radar') >= 0) return;
      var txt = String(text || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      if (!txt || txt.length > 42) return;
      if (/^[\d.,+\-%/\s]+$/.test(txt)) return;

      var sx = Number(rawX) || 0, sy = Number(rawY) || 0;
      if (typeof ctx.getTransform === 'function') {
        try {
          var m = ctx.getTransform();
          if (m) {
            sx = m.a * sx + m.c * sy + m.e;
            sy = m.b * (Number(rawX) || 0) + m.d * sy + m.f;
          }
        } catch (eTr) {}
      }
      var nx = sx / cv.width;
      var ny = sy / cv.height;
      if (!isFinite(nx) || !isFinite(ny) || nx < 0.05 || nx > 0.95 || ny < 0.08 || ny > 0.90) return;

      var now = Date.now();
      var charName = (A.st && A.st.ten) ? noAccent(String(A.st.ten)) : '';
      var tNA = noAccent(txt);
      if (charName && (tNA === charName || (charName.length >= 3 && tNA.indexOf(charName) >= 0))) {
        O.obsPlayer = { nx: nx, ny: ny, at: now };
        return;
      }
      if (tNA.indexOf('boss') >= 0 || tNA.indexOf('hoang kim') >= 0 || tNA.indexOf('thu linh') >= 0 ||
          tNA.indexOf('ho phap') >= 0 || tNA.indexOf('tang ') >= 0 || /^\[.*boss.*\]/i.test(tNA)) {
        O.obsTarget = { nx: nx, ny: ny, at: now };
      }
    } catch (eSpy) {}
  }

  function classifyMinimapFillColor(fs) {
    if (!fs || typeof fs !== 'string') return '';
    var s = fs.toLowerCase().trim();
    if (s.indexOf('#ef4444') >= 0 || s.indexOf('#dc2626') >= 0 || s.indexOf('#ff0000') >= 0 ||
        s.indexOf('#f87171') >= 0 || s.indexOf('#f97316') >= 0 || /^#f[0-4][0-4]$/.test(s)) {
      return 'target';
    }
    if (s.indexOf('#22c55e') >= 0 || s.indexOf('#10b981') >= 0 || s.indexOf('#38bdf8') >= 0 ||
        s.indexOf('#00ff') >= 0 || s.indexOf('#4ade80') >= 0 || s.indexOf('#facc15') >= 0 ||
        s === '#ffffff' || s === '#fff' || s === 'lime' || s === 'cyan' || s === 'white') {
      return 'player';
    }
    var m = /rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)/.exec(s);
    if (m) {
      var r = Number(m[1]), g = Number(m[2]), b = Number(m[3]);
      if (r >= 200 && g <= 95 && b <= 95) return 'target';
      if ((g >= 185 && r <= 120) || (b >= 200 && g >= 160 && r <= 100) || (r >= 235 && g >= 235 && b >= 235)) return 'player';
    }
    return '';
  }

  function handleCanvasSpyDot(ctx, rawX, rawY, sizeW, sizeH) {
    try {
      if (!ctx || !ctx.canvas) return;
      var cv = ctx.canvas;
      var idCls = ((cv.id || '') + ' ' + (typeof cv.className === 'string' ? cv.className : '')).toLowerCase();
      var isMini = (idCls.indexOf('minimap') >= 0 || idCls.indexOf('radar') >= 0) ||
        (cv !== O.targetEl && cv.width >= 40 && cv.width <= 260 && cv.height >= 40 && cv.height <= 260);
      if (!isMini) return;
      if ((sizeW || 0) > 16 || (sizeH || 0) > 16) return;
      var role = classifyMinimapFillColor(ctx.fillStyle);
      if (!role) return;

      var sx = Number(rawX) || 0, sy = Number(rawY) || 0;
      if (typeof ctx.getTransform === 'function') {
        try {
          var m = ctx.getTransform();
          if (m) {
            sx = m.a * sx + m.c * sy + m.e;
            sy = m.b * (Number(rawX) || 0) + m.d * sy + m.f;
          }
        } catch (eTr) {}
      }
      var nx = sx / cv.width;
      var ny = sy / cv.height;
      if (!isFinite(nx) || !isFinite(ny) || nx < 0.02 || nx > 0.98 || ny < 0.02 || ny > 0.98) return;
      var now = Date.now();
      if (role === 'player') O.obsMiniPlayer = { nx: nx, ny: ny, at: now };
      else if (role === 'target') O.obsMiniTarget = { nx: nx, ny: ny, at: now };
    } catch (eDot) {}
  }

  function installArenaCanvasSpy() {
    window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb = {
      text: handleCanvasSpyText,
      dot: handleCanvasSpyDot
    };
    if (window.__vlmOrbitCanvasSpyInstalled) return;
    window.__vlmOrbitCanvasSpyInstalled = true;
    try {
      if (typeof CanvasRenderingContext2D === 'undefined' || !CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype) return;
      var proto = CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype;
      var origFillText = proto.fillText;
      var origStrokeText = proto.strokeText;
      var origFillRect = proto.fillRect;
      var origArc = proto.arc;

      if (origFillText) {
        proto.fillText = function (text, x, y) {
          if (window.__vlmOrbitSpyActive && window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb && window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb.text) {
            window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb.text(this, text, x, y);
          }
          return origFillText.apply(this, arguments);
        };
      }
      if (origStrokeText) {
        proto.strokeText = function (text, x, y) {
          if (window.__vlmOrbitSpyActive && window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb && window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb.text) {
            window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb.text(this, text, x, y);
          }
          return origStrokeText.apply(this, arguments);
        };
      }
      if (origFillRect) {
        proto.fillRect = function (x, y, w, h) {
          if (window.__vlmOrbitSpyActive && window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb && window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb.dot) {
            window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb.dot(this, x + (w || 0) * 0.5, y + (h || 0) * 0.5, w, h);
          }
          return origFillRect.apply(this, arguments);
        };
      }
      if (origArc) {
        proto.arc = function (x, y, r) {
          if (window.__vlmOrbitSpyActive && window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb && window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb.dot) {
            window.__vlmOrbitSpyCb.dot(this, x, y, (r || 0) * 2, (r || 0) * 2);
          }
          return origArc.apply(this, arguments);
        };
      }
    } catch (eHook) {}
  }

  function findGameAutoButton() {
    try {
      var md = document.getElementById('modal');
      var directIds = ['autoBtn', 'bAuto', 'btnAuto', 'u20auto', 'autoAtk', 'atkAuto', 'tAuto', 'autoPlay', 'btnTuDanh', 'tuDanhBtn'];
      for (var i = 0; i < directIds.length; i++) {
        var el = document.getElementById(directIds[i]);
        if (el && isVis(el) && !el.disabled && (!md || !md.contains(el))) return el;
      }
      var attrEls = document.querySelectorAll('[data-act="auto"], [data-cmd="auto"], [data-auto]');
      for (var ai = 0; ai < attrEls.length; ai++) {
        var ae = attrEls[ai];
        if (ae && isVis(ae) && !ae.disabled && (!md || !md.contains(ae)) && ae.id !== 'vlmAuto') return ae;
      }
      var btns = document.querySelectorAll('button, [role="button"], .btn');
      for (var bi = 0; bi < btns.length; bi++) {
        var b = btns[bi];
        if (!b || b.id === 'vlmAuto' || b.id === 'mswBtn' || !isVis(b) || b.disabled) continue;
        if (md && md.contains(b)) continue;
        if (b.closest && b.closest('#modeSw, #mswMenu, #tabs, #t-inv, #t-char, #chatLines, #u20lines, #logBox, #u20log')) continue;
        var raw = (b.textContent || '') + ' ' + (b.getAttribute('title') || '') + ' ' + (b.getAttribute('aria-label') || '');
        var t = noAccent(raw);
        if (!t || t.length > 28) continue;
        if (t.indexOf('che do') >= 0 || t.indexOf('da tau') >= 0 || t.indexOf('sat thu') >= 0 ||
            t.indexOf('hanh trang') >= 0 || t.indexOf('pho ban') >= 0 || t.indexOf('nhiem vu') >= 0 ||
            t.indexOf('leo thap') >= 0 || t.indexOf('luyen cong') >= 0 || t.indexOf('ky tran') >= 0 ||
            t.indexOf('vlm') >= 0) continue;
        if (t === 'auto' || t.indexOf('auto ') === 0 || t.indexOf('auto:') === 0 ||
            t === 'tu danh' || t.indexOf('tu danh') === 0 || t === 'tu chien' ||
            t.indexOf('tu chien') === 0 || t.indexOf('auto danh') >= 0 ||
            t === 'bat auto' || t === 'tat auto' || t === 'dang auto') {
          return b;
        }
      }
    } catch (eAutoBtn) {}
    return null;
  }

  function autoBtnVisualSignature(btn) {
    if (!btn) return '';
    var bg = '';
    try {
      var cs = window.getComputedStyle ? window.getComputedStyle(btn) : null;
      if (cs) bg = (cs.backgroundColor || '') + '|' + (cs.color || '') + '|' + (cs.borderColor || '') + '|' + (cs.opacity || '');
    } catch (eCs) {}
    var inlineStyle = (btn.getAttribute && btn.getAttribute('style')) || '';
    return String(btn.className || '') + '||' + String(inlineStyle) + '||' + noAccent(btn.textContent || '') + '||' + bg;
  }

  function detectGameAutoState(btn) {
    try {
      if (A.st && Date.now() - A.stT < 15000) {
        if (typeof A.st.auto === 'boolean') return A.st.auto ? 'on' : 'off';
        if (typeof A.st.tuDanh === 'boolean') return A.st.tuDanh ? 'on' : 'off';
        if (typeof A.st.autoAtk === 'boolean') return A.st.autoAtk ? 'on' : 'off';
      }
      if (A.info) {
        if (typeof A.info.auto === 'boolean') return A.info.auto ? 'on' : 'off';
        if (typeof A.info.tuDanh === 'boolean') return A.info.tuDanh ? 'on' : 'off';
      }
      if (!btn) return 'unknown';

      var ap = btn.getAttribute && btn.getAttribute('aria-pressed');
      if (ap === 'true') return 'on';
      if (ap === 'false') return 'off';

      var ac = btn.getAttribute && btn.getAttribute('aria-checked');
      if (ac === 'true') return 'on';
      if (ac === 'false') return 'off';

      var dataFlags = ['data-on', 'data-active', 'data-auto'];
      for (var di = 0; di < dataFlags.length; di++) {
        var dv = btn.getAttribute && btn.getAttribute(dataFlags[di]);
        if (dv === '1' || dv === 'true') return 'on';
        if (dv === '0' || dv === 'false') return 'off';
      }

      var t = noAccent(btn.textContent || '');
      if (/(?:^|\b)(auto\s*:?\s*(bat|on)|dang\s*auto|tu\s*danh\s*:?\s*(bat|on)|tat\s*auto)(?:\b|$)/.test(t)) return 'on';
      if (/(?:^|\b)(auto\s*:?\s*(tat|off)|tu\s*danh\s*:?\s*(tat|off)|bat\s*auto)(?:\b|$)/.test(t)) return 'off';

      var cl = btn.classList;
      if (cl) {
        if (cl.contains('on') || cl.contains('active') || cl.contains('checked') || cl.contains('enabled') || cl.contains('is-on') || cl.contains('btn-on')) {
          return 'on';
        }
        if (cl.contains('off') || cl.contains('inactive') || cl.contains('is-off') || cl.contains('btn-off')) {
          return 'off';
        }
      }

      if (O.autoSawOn && O.autoPreMoveSig) {
        var curSig = autoBtnVisualSignature(btn);
        if (curSig && curSig !== O.autoPreMoveSig) return 'off';
      }
    } catch (eDet) {}
    return 'unknown';
  }

  function ensureGameAutoOn(afterMovePulse) {
    try {
      if (O.moving) return false;
      var now = Date.now();
      if (now - O.lastAutoRestoreAt < 450) return false;

      var btn = findGameAutoButton();
      var state = detectGameAutoState(btn);
      if (state === 'on') {
        O.autoSawOn = true;
        if (btn) O.autoPreMoveSig = autoBtnVisualSignature(btn);
        return false;
      }

      if (btn && state === 'off') {
        O.lastAutoRestoreAt = now;
        O.autoPauseUntil = now + 130;
        try { btn.click(); } catch (eClk) {}
        return true;
      }
    } catch (eEns) {}
    return false;
  }

  function dispatchOrbitKey(type, kDef, target) {
    try {
      var ev = new KeyboardEvent(type, {
        key: kDef.key,
        code: kDef.code,
        keyCode: kDef.keyCode,
        which: kDef.keyCode,
        bubbles: true,
        cancelable: true
      });
      var dest = (target && target.isConnected) ? target : (document.body || document);
      dest.dispatchEvent(ev);
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  function syncOrbitKeys(nextState, keyTarget) {
    var dirs = ['up', 'down', 'left', 'right'];
    for (var i = 0; i < dirs.length; i++) {
      var d = dirs[i];
      var want = !!(nextState && nextState[d]);
      if (O.held[d] !== want) {
        O.held[d] = want;
        var grp = ORBIT_KEY_GROUPS[d];
        for (var j = 0; j < grp.length; j++) {
          dispatchOrbitKey(want ? 'keydown' : 'keyup', grp[j], keyTarget);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  function pauseOrbitMovement() {
    if (O.moveEndTimer) {
      clearTimeout(O.moveEndTimer);
      O.moveEndTimer = 0;
    }
    var tgt = (O.targetEl && O.targetEl.isConnected) ? O.targetEl : (document.body || document);
    syncOrbitKeys({ up: false, down: false, left: false, right: false }, tgt);
    if (O.joyPtrActive) {
      O.joyPtrActive = false;
      try {
        var joyEl = findVisibleJoystick();
        if (joyEl) {
          var jr = joyEl.getBoundingClientRect();
          var jx = jr.left + jr.width * 0.5;
          var jy = jr.top + jr.height * 0.5;
          dispatchOrbitPointer(joyEl, 'pointerup', jx, jy);
          dispatchOrbitPointer(joyEl, 'mouseup', jx, jy);
        }
      } catch (eJoy) {}
    }
    O.moving = false;
    O.moveStartedAt = 0;
  }

  function releaseOrbitKeys() {
    pauseOrbitMovement();
    window.__vlmOrbitSpyActive = false;
    O.active = false;
    O.wasInTower = false;
    O.towerEnteredAt = 0;
    O.lastAt = 0;
    O.phaseStart = 0;
    O.lastBeatIdx = -1;
    O.estX = 0;
    O.estY = 0;
    O.lastMoveVx = 0;
    O.lastMoveVy = 0;
    O.autoSawOn = false;
    O.autoPreMoveSig = '';
    O.autoPauseUntil = 0;
  }

  window.__vlmOrbitRelease = releaseOrbitKeys;

  function matchSkillInList(normTxt, list) {
    if (!normTxt) return null;
    for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      if (normTxt === list[i].key) return list[i];
    }
    for (var j = 0; j < list.length; j++) {
      if (normTxt.indexOf(list[j].key) >= 0) return list[j];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function resolveOrbitStyle() {
    var cfgStyle = normalizeOrbitStyleJs(A.cfg.thapNeDonStyle);
    var cleanedSkill = String(A.cfg.thapMainSkill || '').replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, ' ');
    var chosenRaw = noAccent(cleanedSkill);
    var mChosen = chosenRaw ? matchSkillInList(chosenRaw, ORBIT_MELEE_SKILLS) : null;
    var rChosen = chosenRaw ? matchSkillInList(chosenRaw, ORBIT_RANGED_SKILLS) : null;

    if (cfgStyle === 'MELEE') {
      return { style: 'melee', skill: (mChosen || rChosen) ? (mChosen || rChosen).label : '' };
    }
    if (cfgStyle === 'RANGED') {
      return { style: 'ranged', skill: (mChosen || rChosen) ? (mChosen || rChosen).label : '' };
    }

    // Chỉ xác định cách di chuyển theo TÊN CHIÊU CHÍNH 9x người chơi đã chọn
    if (mChosen) return { style: 'melee', skill: mChosen.label };
    if (rChosen) return { style: 'ranged', skill: rChosen.label };
    return { style: '', skill: '' };
  }

  function isInTowerNow() {
    try {
      if (A.st && Date.now() - A.stT < 15000) {
        if (A.st.o && A.st.o !== 'game') return false;
        if (A.st.dead || (typeof A.st.hp === 'number' && A.st.hp <= 0)) return false;
        if (A.st.noi) return A.st.noi === 'thap';
      }
      if (A.info) {
        if (A.info.dead) return false;
        if (A.info.tower === true) return true;
      }
      var mBtn = document.getElementById('mswBtn');
      var mTxt = mBtn ? noAccent(mBtn.textContent || '') : '';
      if (mTxt.indexOf('leo thap') >= 0) {
        var stg = document.getElementById('stageLbl');
        var stTxt = stg ? noAccent(stg.textContent || '') : '';
        if (stTxt.indexOf('chua di') >= 0) return false;
        if (stTxt.indexOf('tang') >= 0 || stTxt.indexOf('thap') >= 0 || (A.info && A.info.mode === 'tower')) {
          return true;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    return false;
  }

  function isOrbitEligible() {
    if (!A.cfg.on || !A.cfg.thapNeDon) return false;
    if (window.__isUserTouching) return false;
    if (Date.now() - A.userAt <= 2500) return false;
    try {
      var ae = document.activeElement;
      if (ae) {
        var tg = (ae.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
        if (tg === 'INPUT' || tg === 'TEXTAREA' || tg === 'SELECT') return false;
      }
      if (isModalLocked() || document.documentElement.classList.contains('__vlm-stealth')) return false;
      if (isAuthOrProtectedModalOpen()) return false;
      var md = document.getElementById('modal');
      if (md && !md.classList.contains('hidden')) return false;
      var popIds = ['mswMenu', 'cfModal', 'confirmModal', 'cModal', 'itemModal', 'detailModal'];
      for (var pi = 0; pi < popIds.length; pi++) {
        var pe = document.getElementById(popIds[pi]);
        if (pe && !pe.classList.contains('hidden') && isVis(pe)) return false;
      }
    } catch (e) { return false; }
    return isInTowerNow();
  }

  function findPrimaryGameCanvas() {
    var best = null;
    var bestArea = 0;
    try {
      var canvases = document.querySelectorAll('canvas');
      for (var i = 0; i < canvases.length; i++) {
        var c = canvases[i];
        if (!isVis(c)) continue;
        if (c.closest && c.closest('#modal, #minimap, .minimap')) continue;
        var rect = c.getBoundingClientRect ? c.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
        if (!rect || rect.width < 80 || rect.height < 80) continue;
        var area = rect.width * rect.height;
        if (area > bestArea) {
          bestArea = area;
          best = { canvas: c, rect: rect };
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    return best;
  }

  function findVisibleJoystick() {
    try {
      var sels = ['#joy', '#joystick', '#vpad', '.joystick', '.virtual-joystick', '[data-joystick]'];
      for (var i = 0; i < sels.length; i++) {
        var el = document.querySelector(sels[i]);
        if (el && isVis(el) && el.getBoundingClientRect) {
          var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
          if (r && r.width >= 36 && r.height >= 36) return el;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    return null;
  }

  function computeOrbitParams(style) {
    // Nhịp ngắn né quanh trung tâm map + khoảng dừng dài với Auto BẬT để nhân vật tung chiêu tấn công mục tiêu
    if (style === 'melee') {
      return {
        omega: 1.15,
        cycleMs: 900,
        moveMs: 210,
        rTarget: 0.085,
        rMax: 0.13,
        estSpeed: 0.42
      };
    }
    return {
      omega: 0.85,
      cycleMs: 900,
      moveMs: 250,
      rTarget: 0.16,
      rMax: 0.23,
      estSpeed: 0.44
    };
  }

  function readStateCoords() {
    var srcs = [];
    if (A.st && Date.now() - A.stT < 15000) srcs.push(A.st);
    if (A.info) srcs.push(A.info);
    for (var i = 0; i < srcs.length; i++) {
      var s = srcs[i];
      if (!s || typeof s !== 'object') continue;
      var px = typeof s.x === 'number' ? s.x : (typeof s.px === 'number' ? s.px : null);
      var py = typeof s.y === 'number' ? s.y : (typeof s.py === 'number' ? s.py : null);
      var mw = typeof s.mapW === 'number' ? s.mapW : (typeof s.w === 'number' ? s.w : null);
      var mh = typeof s.mapH === 'number' ? s.mapH : (typeof s.h === 'number' ? s.h : null);
      if (px !== null && py !== null && mw && mh && mw > 0 && mh > 0) {
        return {
          px: (px / mw) - 0.5,
          py: (py / mh) - 0.5,
          cx: 0,
          cy: 0,
          source: 'state'
        };
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  function resolveArenaPosition(now) {
    var stPos = readStateCoords();
    if (stPos) return stPos;

    if (O.obsMiniPlayer && now - O.obsMiniPlayer.at < 1500) {
      var mcx = 0, mcy = 0;
      if (O.obsMiniTarget && now - O.obsMiniTarget.at < 1500) {
        // Giữ điểm tập trung neo vào trung tâm map, chỉ dịch nhẹ theo boss nếu boss ở gần trung tâm
        var mtx = O.obsMiniTarget.nx - 0.5;
        var mty = O.obsMiniTarget.ny - 0.5;
        if (Math.hypot(mtx, mty) <= 0.22) {
          mcx = mtx * 0.5;
          mcy = mty * 0.5;
        }
      }
      return {
        px: O.obsMiniPlayer.nx - 0.5,
        py: O.obsMiniPlayer.ny - 0.5,
        cx: mcx,
        cy: mcy,
        source: 'minimap'
      };
    }

    if (O.obsPlayer && O.obsTarget && now - O.obsPlayer.at < 1200 && now - O.obsTarget.at < 1200) {
      return {
        px: O.obsPlayer.nx - O.obsTarget.nx,
        py: O.obsPlayer.ny - O.obsTarget.ny,
        cx: 0,
        cy: 0,
        source: 'canvas_target'
      };
    }

    if (O.obsPlayer && now - O.obsPlayer.at < 1200) {
      var offX = O.obsPlayer.nx - 0.5;
      var offY = O.obsPlayer.ny - 0.5;
      if (Math.hypot(offX, offY) > 0.03) {
        return {
          px: offX,
          py: offY,
          cx: 0,
          cy: 0,
          source: 'canvas_center'
        };
      }
    }

    return {
      px: O.estX,
      py: O.estY,
      cx: 0,
      cy: 0,
      source: 'center_anchor'
    };
  }

  function computeCenterAnchoredVector(pos, params, theta) {
    var dxCenter = pos.px - pos.cx;
    var dyCenter = pos.py - pos.cy;
    var distCenter = Math.hypot(dxCenter, dyCenter);

    if (distCenter > params.rMax && distCenter > 0.0001) {
      // Kéo thẳng về tọa độ trung tâm map để không đi quá xa mục tiêu
      return {
        vx: -dxCenter / distCenter,
        vy: -dyCenter / distCenter,
        clamped: true
      };
    }

    var goalX = pos.cx + params.rTarget * Math.cos(theta);
    var goalY = pos.cy + params.rTarget * Math.sin(theta);
    var errX = goalX - pos.px;
    var errY = goalY - pos.py;
    var errLen = Math.hypot(errX, errY);
    if (errLen < 0.0001) {
      return {
        vx: -Math.sin(theta),
        vy: Math.cos(theta),
        clamped: false
      };
    }
    return {
      vx: errX / errLen,
      vy: errY / errLen,
      clamped: false
    };
  }

  function dispatchOrbitPointer(target, type, x, y) {
    try {
      var pEv = (typeof PointerEvent === 'function')
        ? new PointerEvent(type, {
            clientX: x,
            clientY: y,
            bubbles: true,
            cancelable: true,
            button: 0,
            buttons: type === 'pointerup' || type === 'mouseup' ? 0 : 1,
            pointerId: 1,
            pointerType: 'touch',
            isPrimary: true
          })
        : null;
      if (pEv && type.indexOf('pointer') === 0) {
        target.dispatchEvent(pEv);
        return;
      }
      var mEv = new MouseEvent(type, {
        clientX: x,
        clientY: y,
        bubbles: true,
        cancelable: true,
        button: 0,
        buttons: type === 'mouseup' ? 0 : 1
      });
      target.dispatchEvent(mEv);
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  function pulseJoystickOrbit(vx, vy, joyEl) {
    var joy = joyEl || findVisibleJoystick();
    if (!joy) return false;
    var jr = joy.getBoundingClientRect();
    var jcx = jr.left + jr.width * 0.5;
    var jcy = jr.top + jr.height * 0.5;
    var jRad = Math.min(jr.width, jr.height) * 0.35;
    var jx = jcx + vx * jRad;
    var jy = jcy + vy * jRad;
    if (!O.joyPtrActive) {
      O.joyPtrActive = true;
      dispatchOrbitPointer(joy, 'pointerdown', jcx, jcy);
      dispatchOrbitPointer(joy, 'mousedown', jcx, jcy);
    }
    dispatchOrbitPointer(joy, 'pointermove', jx, jy);
    dispatchOrbitPointer(joy, 'mousemove', jx, jy);
    return true;
  }

  function finishOrbitMovePulse() {
    if (!O.moving) return;
    var now = Date.now();
    var moveSec = O.moveStartedAt > 0 ? Math.min(0.35, Math.max(0.05, (now - O.moveStartedAt) / 1000)) : 0.2;
    var params = computeOrbitParams(O.style || 'ranged');
    O.estX += O.lastMoveVx * params.estSpeed * moveSec;
    O.estY += O.lastMoveVy * params.estSpeed * moveSec;
    pauseOrbitMovement();
    O.lastAt = now;
    ensureGameAutoOn(true);
  }

  function orbitTick() {
    if (!A.cfg.on || !A.cfg.thapNeDon) {
      if (O.active || O.moving || O.wasInTower || O.held.up || O.held.down || O.held.left || O.held.right || O.joyPtrActive) {
        releaseOrbitKeys();
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!isOrbitEligible()) {
      if (O.active || O.moving || O.wasInTower || O.held.up || O.held.down || O.held.left || O.held.right || O.joyPtrActive) {
        releaseOrbitKeys();
      }
      return;
    }

    var stage = findPrimaryGameCanvas();
    if (!stage) {
      if (O.active || O.moving) releaseOrbitKeys();
      return;
    }

    installArenaCanvasSpy();
    window.__vlmOrbitSpyActive = true;
    O.targetEl = stage.canvas;

    var now = Date.now();
    if (!O.wasInTower) {
      O.wasInTower = true;
      O.towerEnteredAt = now;
      O.estX = 0;
      O.estY = 0;
      O.lastEstDecayAt = now;
    }

    var resolved = resolveOrbitStyle();
    var style = resolved.style;
    if (!style) {
      if (O.active || O.moving) releaseOrbitKeys();
      O.style = '';
      O.skillName = '';
      if (now - O.lastNeedSkillNoteAt > 30000) {
        O.lastNeedSkillNoteAt = now;
        note('ℹ️ Leo Tháp: hãy chọn chiêu chính 9x trong 🤖 Tự động để xác định cách di chuyển');
      }
      return;
    }

    // Chờ ~900ms khi mới vào tầng để Auto tiếp cận khu vực trung tâm map / boss và ghi nhận trạng thái Auto
    if (now - O.towerEnteredAt < 900) {
      var preBtn = findGameAutoButton();
      if (detectGameAutoState(preBtn) === 'on' && preBtn) {
        O.autoSawOn = true;
        O.autoPreMoveSig = autoBtnVisualSignature(preBtn);
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!O.phaseStart) {
      O.phaseStart = now;
      O.lastBeatIdx = -1;
    }

    var params = computeOrbitParams(style);
    var elapsedTotal = Math.max(0, now - O.phaseStart);
    var beatIdx = Math.floor(elapsedTotal / params.cycleMs);
    var elapsedInBeat = elapsedTotal % params.cycleMs;
    var isMoveBeat = elapsedInBeat < params.moveMs;

    O.style = style;
    O.skillName = resolved.skill || '';

    if (!isMoveBeat) {
      if (O.moving) {
        finishOrbitMovePulse();
      } else {
        // Trong nhịp dừng đánh: luôn giữ Auto game BẬT để nhân vật khóa mục tiêu & tung chiêu
        if (O.lastEstDecayAt > 0 && now - O.lastEstDecayAt >= 180) {
          O.estX *= 0.86;
          O.estY *= 0.86;
          O.lastEstDecayAt = now;
        }
        ensureGameAutoOn(false);
      }
      O.lastAt = now;
      O.active = true;
      return;
    }

    if (now < O.autoPauseUntil) {
      return;
    }

    // Mỗi nhịp chỉ tạo 1 bước né ngắn theo mục tiêu vòng tròn quanh trung tâm map
    if (O.moving && beatIdx === O.lastBeatIdx) {
      if (O.moveStartedAt > 0 && now - O.moveStartedAt >= params.moveMs) {
        finishOrbitMovePulse();
      }
      return;
    }

    if (beatIdx === O.lastBeatIdx) {
      return;
    }

    O.lastBeatIdx = beatIdx;

    // Cập nhật góc quỹ đạo tiến dần theo từng nhịp vòng quanh trung tâm map
    O.theta = (O.theta + params.omega * (params.cycleMs / 1000)) % (Math.PI * 2);

    var pos = resolveArenaPosition(now);
    if (pos.source !== 'center_anchor') {
      O.estX = pos.px - pos.cx;
      O.estY = pos.py - pos.cy;
    }

    var steer = computeCenterAnchoredVector(pos, params, O.theta);
    O.lastMoveVx = steer.vx;
    O.lastMoveVy = steer.vy;
    O.moveStartedAt = now;
    O.lastAt = now;

    // Ghi nhận chữ ký nút Auto trước khi gửi xung di chuyển
    var autoBtnBefore = findGameAutoButton();
    if (detectGameAutoState(autoBtnBefore) === 'on' && autoBtnBefore) {
      O.autoSawOn = true;
      O.autoPreMoveSig = autoBtnVisualSignature(autoBtnBefore);
    }

    // Chỉ dùng 1 kênh di chuyển sạch (joystick nếu hiện rõ, ngược lại phím mũi tên) để tránh xung đột
    var joyEl = findVisibleJoystick();
    if (joyEl) {
      syncOrbitKeys({ up: false, down: false, left: false, right: false }, stage.canvas);
      pulseJoystickOrbit(steer.vx, steer.vy, joyEl);
    } else {
      syncOrbitKeys({
        up: steer.vy < -0.35,
        down: steer.vy > 0.35,
        left: steer.vx < -0.35,
        right: steer.vx > 0.35
      }, stage.canvas);
    }

    O.moving = true;
    if (O.moveEndTimer) clearTimeout(O.moveEndTimer);
    O.moveEndTimer = setTimeout(function () {
      try { finishOrbitMovePulse(); } catch (eFin) {}
    }, params.moveMs);

    if (!O.active) {
      O.active = true;
      if (now - O.lastNoteAt > 20000) {
        O.lastNoteAt = now;
        var skInfo = O.skillName ? (' · ' + O.skillName) : '';
        note('🌀 Leo Tháp: neo trung tâm map + giữ Auto đánh (' + (style === 'melee' ? 'Đánh gần' : 'Đánh xa') + skInfo + ')');
      }
    }
  }

  window.__vlmAuto = {
    ver: VER,
    setCfg: setCfg,
    resetDay: resetDay,
    onMidnight000000: onMidnight000000,
    onNewVnDay: onNewVnDay,
    tick: tick,
    orbitTick: orbitTick,
    orbitState: function () {
      return {
        active: O.active,
        moving: O.moving,
        style: O.style,
        skill: O.skillName,
        theta: O.theta,
        estX: O.estX,
        estY: O.estY,
        held: Object.assign({}, O.held)
      };
    },
    trangThai: function () {
      return { on: !!A.cfg.on, busy: A.busy, last: A.last, lastAt: A.lastAt, st: A.st, orbit: { active: O.active, moving: O.moving, style: O.style, skill: O.skillName } };
    }
  };

  setCfg(CFG0, TOUCH0, IMM0);
  if (IMM0 && A.cfg.on) {
    setTimeout(function () { tick().catch(function () {}); }, 250);
  }
  setInterval(function () { tick().catch(function () {}); }, 15000);
  window.__vlmOrbitTimer = setInterval(function () {
    try { orbitTick(); } catch (eOrb) {}
  }, 90);
  return 'installed';
})();
    """.trimIndent()
}

package com.example.webview

import org.json.JSONObject

object GameScriptInjector {

    fun quote(str: String): String {
        return "\"" + str
            .replace("\\", "\\\\")
            .replace("\"", "\\\"")
            .replace("\n", "\\n")
            .replace("\r", "\\r")
            .replace("\t", "\\t")
            .replace("\b", "\\b") + "\""
    }

    fun buildClearStorageScript(): String {
        return """
        try {
            localStorage.clear();
            sessionStorage.clear();
        } catch(e) {}
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    fun buildAdjustViewportScript(): String {
        return """
        try {
            if (!document.getElementById('__vl_mobile_opt_style') && document.head) {
                const st = document.createElement('style');
                st.id = '__vl_mobile_opt_style';
                st.textContent = `
                    *, *::before, *::after {
                        backdrop-filter: none !important;
                        -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important;
                        box-shadow: none !important;
                        text-shadow: none !important;
                    }
                    .floating-text, .damage-text, .damage-number, .particle, .fx {
                        animation: none !important;
                        transition: none !important;
                        filter: none !important;
                    }
                    input:not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="button"]):not([type="submit"]) {
                        min-width: 44px !important;
                    }
                    @media (max-width: 280px) {
                        input:not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="button"]):not([type="submit"]) {
                            min-width: 36px !important;
                            width: 100% !important;
                        }
                    }
                `;
                document.head.appendChild(st);
            }
            window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize'));
            if (document.body) {
                document.body.style.width = '100%';
                document.body.style.height = '100%';
            }
        } catch(e) {}
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    fun buildAutoLoginStepScript(
        tabIndex: Int,
        username: String,
        password: String
    ): String {
        val safeUserJson = quote(username)
        val safePassJson = quote(password)

        return """
        (function(username, password) {
            try {
                const tabIdx = $tabIndex;

                try {
                    if (!document.getElementById('__vl_mobile_opt_style') && document.head) {
                        const st = document.createElement('style');
                        st.id = '__vl_mobile_opt_style';
                        st.textContent = `
                            * {
                                backdrop-filter: none !important;
                                -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important;
                            }
                            input:not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="button"]):not([type="submit"]) {
                                min-width: 44px !important;
                            }
                            @media (max-width: 280px) {
                                input:not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="button"]):not([type="submit"]) {
                                    min-width: 36px !important;
                                    width: 100% !important;
                                }
                            }
                        `;
                        document.head.appendChild(st);
                    }
                } catch(e) {}

                function norm(str) {
                    return (str || '').toString().toLowerCase().normalize('NFC').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                }

                function noAccent(str) {
                    return norm(str)
                        .normalize('NFD')
                        .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
                        .replace(/đ/g, 'd');
                }

                function isVisible(el) {
                    if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
                    if ((el.type || '').toLowerCase() === 'hidden') return false;
                    let cur = el;
                    while (cur && cur !== document.body && cur !== document.documentElement) {
                        if (cur.hidden === true) return false;
                        try {
                            const style = window.getComputedStyle(cur);
                            if (style && (style.display === 'none' || style.visibility === 'hidden' || style.visibility === 'collapse' || parseFloat(style.opacity) === 0)) {
                                return false;
                            }
                        } catch(e) {}
                        cur = cur.parentElement;
                    }
                    const tag = (el.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
                    const isFormOrBtn = (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'BUTTON' || tag === 'SELECT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || el.getAttribute('role') === 'button');
                    try {
                        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
                        if (isFormOrBtn) {
                            if (rect.height > 0 || rect.width > 0 || el.offsetParent !== null) return true;
                            if (el.parentElement) {
                                const pRect = el.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
                                return pRect.height > 0 || pRect.width > 0;
                            }
                        }
                        return rect.width > 1 && rect.height > 1;
                    } catch(e) {
                        return el.offsetParent !== null;
                    }
                }

                function realClick(el) {
                    if (!el) return;
                    const target = el.closest('button, [role="button"], a, input[type="button"], input[type="submit"]') || el;
                    try {
                        target.disabled = false;
                        target.removeAttribute('disabled');
                        if (target.classList) target.classList.remove('disabled');
                    } catch(e) {}
                    try {
                        const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
                        const cx = rect.left + Math.max(2, rect.width / 2);
                        const cy = rect.top + Math.max(2, rect.height / 2);
                        ['pointerdown', 'mousedown', 'pointerup', 'mouseup'].forEach(type => {
                            try {
                                target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(type, {
                                    view: window, bubbles: true, cancelable: true, clientX: cx, clientY: cy, buttons: 1
                                }));
                            } catch(e) {}
                        });
                    } catch(e) {}
                    if (typeof target.click === 'function') {
                        try { target.click(); } catch(e) {}
                    } else {
                        try {
                            target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', {
                                view: window, bubbles: true, cancelable: true
                            }));
                        } catch(e) {}
                    }
                }

                function forceFillReactInput(inputEl, textValue) {
                    if (!inputEl || textValue === undefined || textValue === null) return;
                    const strVal = String(textValue);
                    const prevInputMode = inputEl.getAttribute('inputmode');
                    try {
                        inputEl.setAttribute('inputmode', 'none');
                    } catch(e) {}

                    try { inputEl.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch(e) {
                        try { inputEl.focus(); } catch(e2) {}
                    }

                    if (inputEl._valueTracker) {
                        try { inputEl._valueTracker.setValue(''); } catch(e) {}
                    }

                    let nativeSetter = null;
                    try {
                        nativeSetter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window.HTMLInputElement.prototype, 'value')?.set;
                        if (!nativeSetter) {
                            nativeSetter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(Object.getPrototypeOf(inputEl), 'value')?.set;
                        }
                    } catch(e) {}

                    if (nativeSetter) {
                        try { nativeSetter.call(inputEl, strVal); } catch(e) { inputEl.value = strVal; }
                    } else {
                        inputEl.value = strVal;
                    }

                    try { inputEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('focus', { bubbles: true })); } catch(e) {}
                    try { inputEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('keydown', { bubbles: true })); } catch(e) {}
                    try {
                        if (typeof InputEvent === 'function') {
                            inputEl.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('input', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, data: strVal, inputType: 'insertText' }));
                        } else {
                            inputEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
                        }
                    } catch(e) {
                        try { inputEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); } catch(e2) {}
                    }
                    try { inputEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); } catch(e) {}
                    try { inputEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('keyup', { bubbles: true })); } catch(e) {}
                    try { inputEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('blur', { bubbles: true })); } catch(e) {}

                    setTimeout(function() {
                        try {
                            if (prevInputMode === null) inputEl.removeAttribute('inputmode');
                            else inputEl.setAttribute('inputmode', prevInputMode);
                        } catch(e) {}
                    }, 600);
                }

                function getOwnInputMeta(inp) {
                    const parts = [
                        inp.placeholder || '',
                        inp.name || '',
                        inp.id || '',
                        inp.getAttribute('aria-label') || '',
                        inp.getAttribute('autocomplete') || '',
                        inp.title || ''
                    ];
                    if (inp.labels && inp.labels.length > 0) {
                        for (const lb of Array.from(inp.labels)) {
                            parts.push(lb.textContent || lb.innerText || '');
                        }
                    }
                    const closestLabel = inp.closest('label');
                    if (closestLabel) {
                        parts.push(closestLabel.textContent || closestLabel.innerText || '');
                    }
                    const prev = inp.previousElementSibling;
                    if (prev) {
                        const prevTxt = (prev.textContent || prev.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                        if (prevTxt.length <= 50) parts.push(prevTxt);
                    }
                    const parent = inp.parentElement;
                    if (parent) {
                        const inputsInParent = parent.querySelectorAll('input').length;
                        if (inputsInParent === 1) {
                            const pTxt = (parent.textContent || parent.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                            if (pTxt.length <= 70) {
                                parts.push(pTxt);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    return noAccent(parts.join(' '));
                }

                function isServerField(inp) {
                    if ((inp.type || '').toLowerCase() === 'password') return false;
                    if (inp.readOnly || inp.disabled) return true;
                    const valNA = noAccent(inp.value || '');
                    if (valNA.includes('vo lam idle')) return true;
                    const meta = getOwnInputMeta(inp);
                    if (meta.includes('tai khoan') || meta.includes('mat ma') || meta.includes('mat khau') || meta.includes('username') || meta.includes('password')) {
                        return false;
                    }
                    return meta.includes('may chu') || meta.includes('server');
                }

                function isConfirmField(inp) {
                    const meta = getOwnInputMeta(inp);
                    return meta.includes('xac nhan') || meta.includes('nhap lai') || meta.includes('confirm') || meta.includes('retype');
                }

                function isPasswordField(inp) {
                    if (isServerField(inp)) return false;
                    if ((inp.type || '').toLowerCase() === 'password') return true;
                    const meta = getOwnInputMeta(inp);
                    return meta.includes('mat ma') || meta.includes('mat khau') || meta.includes('password') || meta.includes('pass');
                }

                function isUsernameField(inp) {
                    if (isServerField(inp) || isPasswordField(inp) || isConfirmField(inp)) return false;
                    const meta = getOwnInputMeta(inp);
                    return meta.includes('tai khoan') || meta.includes('ten dang nhap') || meta.includes('ten nhan vat') ||
                           meta.includes('username') || meta.includes('account') || meta.includes('user');
                }

                const CHAT_COMPOSER_KEYWORDS = ['nhan tin', 'tin nhan', 'chat', 'nhap tin', 'noi dung', 'message', 'comment', 'mat ngu'];

                function isChatOrComposer(inp) {
                    const id = (inp.id || '').toLowerCase();
                    if (id === 'u20txt' || id === 'chattxt' || id === 'hhchtxt') return true;
                    const meta = getOwnInputMeta(inp);
                    for (const kw of CHAT_COMPOSER_KEYWORDS) {
                        if (meta.includes(kw)) return true;
                    }
                    return false;
                }

                const NON_AUTH_IDS = ['gcin', 'nmto', 'nmin', 'nmnote', 'ptname', 'ptto', 'ptnote',
                                      'hhname', 'hhmnname', 'hhdenname', 'ncname',
                                      'mku', 'mkm', 'mkc', 'mkp1', 'mkp2', 'bstb', 'bstuyen',
                                      'prq', 'hfq', 'nkq', 'petnmi', 'nau', 'nap'];

                function authGateRoot() {
                    return document.querySelector('#modal .dn20[data-dn20], .dn20[data-dn20]');
                }

                function authScopeRoot() {
                    const gate = authGateRoot();
                    if (gate) return gate;
                    if (document.getElementById('naP') || document.getElementById('naIn')) return null;
                    return document;
                }

                const allClickables = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button, input[type="submit"], input[type="button"], [role="button"], a, div, span, p, label'));

                function getVisibleEditableInputs() {
                    const scope = authScopeRoot();
                    if (!scope) return [];
                    return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input')).filter(inp => {
                        if (scope !== document && !scope.contains(inp)) return false;
                        if (NON_AUTH_IDS.indexOf((inp.id || '').toLowerCase()) >= 0) return false;
                        if (inp.classList && inp.classList.contains('bhin')) return false;
                        const t = (inp.type || 'text').toLowerCase();
                        if (['hidden', 'checkbox', 'radio', 'button', 'submit', 'reset', 'image', 'file', 'range', 'color', 'number', 'search', 'date', 'time', 'datetime-local', 'month', 'week'].includes(t)) {
                            return false;
                        }
                        if (!isVisible(inp)) return false;
                        if (isServerField(inp)) return false;
                        if (isChatOrComposer(inp)) return false;
                        return true;
                    });
                }

                function byId(id) {
                    return document.getElementById(id);
                }

                function gateLoginVisible() {
                    const gate = authGateRoot();
                    if (!gate || !isVisible(gate)) return false;
                    const u = byId('ngU');
                    const p = byId('ngP');
                    return !!((u && isVisible(u)) || (p && isVisible(p)));
                }

                function gateMenuVisible() {
                    const b = byId('ngStart');
                    return !!(b && isVisible(b));
                }

                function charSelectVisible() {
                    const c = byId('csel');
                    return !!(c && isVisible(c));
                }

                function inGameNow() {
                    if (gateLoginVisible() || gateMenuVisible() || charSelectVisible()) return false;
                    const msw = byId('modeSw');
                    if (msw && !msw.classList.contains('hidden')) return true;
                    const body = document.body;
                    if (body && (body.classList.contains('u20') || body.classList.contains('u2m'))) return true;
                    const stage = byId('stageLbl');
                    if (stage && (stage.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, '') !== '') return true;
                    const logBox = byId('logBox');
                    if (logBox && logBox.children && logBox.children.length > 0) return true;
                    return false;
                }

                function findGateSubmitButton() {
                    const gate = authGateRoot();
                    const btn = byId('ngIn');
                    if (btn && isVisible(btn) && (!gate || gate.contains(btn))) return btn;
                    return null;
                }

                function ensureRememberAccount() {
                    const rem = byId('ngRem');
                    if (rem) {
                        if (!rem.classList.contains('on')) {
                            try { rem.click(); } catch(e) {}
                        }
                        return;
                    }
                    const visibleCheckboxes = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]')).filter(isVisible);
                    for (const cb of visibleCheckboxes) {
                        const lb = cb.closest('label');
                        const nextEl = cb.nextElementSibling;
                        const parentEl = cb.parentElement;
                        const singleCbInParent = parentEl && parentEl.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]').length === 1;
                        const cbText = noAccent(
                            (lb ? (lb.textContent || lb.innerText || '') : '') + ' ' +
                            (nextEl ? (nextEl.textContent || nextEl.innerText || '') : '') + ' ' +
                            (singleCbInParent ? (parentEl.textContent || parentEl.innerText || '') : '')
                        );

                        if (cbText.includes('hien mat') || cbText.includes('hien thi mat') || cbText.includes('show pass')) {
                            if (cb.checked) {
                                try { cb.click(); } catch(e) {}
                            }
                            continue;
                        }

                        if (cbText.includes('nho tai khoan') || cbText.includes('ghi nho') || cbText.includes('remember')) {
                            if (!cb.checked) {
                                try { cb.click(); } catch(e) {}
                                if (!cb.checked) {
                                    cb.checked = true;
                                    try { cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); } catch(e) {}
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                function isBtnBusyOrDisabled(btn) {
                    if (!btn) return false;
                    try {
                        if (btn.disabled || btn.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true' ||
                            (btn.classList && (btn.classList.contains('disabled') || btn.classList.contains('loading')))) {
                            return true;
                        }
                        const t = noAccent(btn.innerText || btn.textContent || btn.value || '');
                        if (t.includes('dang ket noi') || t.includes('dang dang nhap') ||
                            t.includes('dang xu ly') || t.includes('dang tai') ||
                            t.includes('connecting') || t.includes('loading')) {
                            return true;
                        }
                    } catch(e) {}
                    return false;
                }

                function tryEnterCharacterSelect() {
                    const csel = byId('csel');
                    if (!csel || !isVisible(csel)) return null;
                    const go = byId('csGo');
                    if (!go || !isVisible(go) || isBtnBusyOrDisabled(go)) {
                        if (Date.now() - (window.__lastCharPickTime || 0) >= 2200) {
                            const cards = csel.querySelectorAll('.cs-slot[data-i]:not(.empty)');
                            for (let i = 0; i < cards.length; i++) {
                                const card = cards[i];
                                if (card.classList.contains('on')) continue;
                                window.__lastCharPickTime = Date.now();
                                realClick(card);
                                return 'CLICKED_CHARACTER_CARD';
                            }
                        }
                        return 'WAITING_CHAR_SELECT';
                    }
                    if (Date.now() - (window.__lastCharGoTime || 0) < 4500) return 'WAITING_CHAR_SELECT';

                    const onCard = csel.querySelector('.cs-slot.on');
                    if (!onCard && Date.now() - (window.__lastCharPickTime || 0) >= 2200) {
                        const firstCard = csel.querySelector('.cs-slot[data-i]:not(.empty)');
                        if (firstCard) {
                            window.__lastCharPickTime = Date.now();
                            realClick(firstCard);
                            return 'CLICKED_CHARACTER_CARD';
                        }
                    }

                    window.__lastCharGoTime = Date.now();
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        try {
                            const btn = byId('csGo');
                            if (btn && !isBtnBusyOrDisabled(btn) && isVisible(btn)) realClick(btn);
                        } catch(e) {}
                    }, 350);

                    if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                        window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog(tabIdx, "Đã chọn nhân vật và ấn [Xác Định] vào game");
                    }
                    return "CLICKED_CHARACTER_SELECT";
                }

                function findBestButtonByText(matcherFn) {
                    let bestEl = null;
                    let bestScore = -999;

                    for (const el of allClickables) {
                        const rawContent = (el.textContent || el.value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                        if (!rawContent || rawContent.length > 45) continue;
                        const contentNA = noAccent(rawContent);

                        let matchScore = matcherFn(contentNA, rawContent, el);
                        if (matchScore <= 0) {
                            const rawInner = (el.textContent || el.value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                            if (rawInner && rawInner.length <= 45 && rawInner !== rawContent) {
                                matchScore = matcherFn(noAccent(rawInner), rawInner, el);
                            }
                        }
                        if (matchScore <= 0) continue;

                        if (!isVisible(el)) continue;

                        let score = matchScore;
                        const tag = (el.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
                        if (tag === 'BUTTON' || tag === 'INPUT') score += 60;
                        else if (el.getAttribute('role') === 'button' || tag === 'A') score += 45;
                        else if (el.closest('button, [role="button"], a')) score += 35;

                        if (el.children && el.children.length === 0) score += 20;
                        else if (el.children && el.children.length <= 4) score += 10;

                        if (score > bestScore) {
                            bestScore = score;
                            bestEl = el;
                        }
                    }
                    return bestEl;
                }

                function findLoginSubmitButton(referenceInput) {
                    const refRect = referenceInput ? referenceInput.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
                    return findBestButtonByText(function(txtNA, rawTxt, el) {
                        if (txtNA === 'huy' || txtNA.includes('dang ky') || txtNA.includes('quen mat') ||
                            txtNA.includes('hien mat') || txtNA.includes('nho tai khoan') ||
                            txtNA.includes('tao nhan vat') || txtNA.includes('chua co tai khoan') ||
                            txtNA.includes('bat dau tro choi')) {
                            return -1;
                        }

                        let base = 0;
                        if (txtNA === 'xac dinh') base = 200;
                        else if (txtNA === 'xac nhan' || txtNA === 'dang nhap' || txtNA === 'login') base = 120;
                        else if (txtNA.startsWith('xac dinh') || txtNA.startsWith('dang nhap')) base = 90;
                        else return -1;

                        if (referenceInput && refRect && refRect.height > 0) {
                            try {
                                const btnRect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
                                if (btnRect.height > 0 && btnRect.top >= refRect.top - 20) {
                                    base += 80;
                                }
                            } catch(e) {}
                        }
                        return base;
                    });
                }

                function tryFillAndSubmitLoginModal() {
                    const editableInputs = getVisibleEditableInputs();
                    if (editableInputs.length === 0) return null;

                    let passInput = null;
                    let userInput = null;

                    for (const inp of editableInputs) {
                        if ((inp.type || '').toLowerCase() === 'password' && !isConfirmField(inp)) {
                            passInput = inp;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (!passInput) {
                        for (const inp of editableInputs) {
                            if (isPasswordField(inp) && !isConfirmField(inp)) {
                                passInput = inp;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (!passInput && editableInputs.length === 2 && !isConfirmField(editableInputs[1]) && authGateRoot()) {
                        userInput = editableInputs[0];
                        passInput = editableInputs[1];
                    }

                    if (!passInput) return null;

                    if (!username || !password) {
                        if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                            window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog(tabIdx, "Chờ nhập TK & MK ở nút 🔑 TK");
                        }
                        return "NO_CREDENTIALS";
                    }

                    if (!userInput) {
                        for (const inp of editableInputs) {
                            if (inp === passInput) continue;
                            if (isUsernameField(inp)) {
                                userInput = inp;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (!userInput) {
                        const passIdx = editableInputs.indexOf(passInput);
                        if (passIdx > 0) {
                            for (let i = passIdx - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                                const candidate = editableInputs[i];
                                if (!isConfirmField(candidate) && !isPasswordField(candidate)) {
                                    userInput = candidate;
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (!userInput) {
                        for (const inp of editableInputs) {
                            if (inp !== passInput && !isConfirmField(inp)) {
                                userInput = inp;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    const nowMs = Date.now();
                    const timeSinceLastSubmit = nowMs - (window.__lastLoginSubmitTime || 0);
                    const valuesAlreadyMatch = (!userInput || userInput.value === username) && (passInput.value === password);
                    const initialSubmitBtn = findGateSubmitButton() || findLoginSubmitButton(passInput);

                    if (isBtnBusyOrDisabled(initialSubmitBtn)) {
                        return "WAITING_LOGIN_RESPONSE";
                    }

                    if (valuesAlreadyMatch && timeSinceLastSubmit < 4500) {
                        return "WAITING_LOGIN_RESPONSE";
                    }

                    window.__lastLoginSubmitTime = nowMs;

                    if (userInput && userInput.value !== username) {
                        forceFillReactInput(userInput, username);
                    }
                    if (passInput.value !== password) {
                        forceFillReactInput(passInput, password);
                    }

                    ensureRememberAccount();

                    function confirmAndClickSubmit() {
                        try {
                            if (!passInput || !passInput.isConnected || !isVisible(passInput)) return;
                            if (userInput && userInput.isConnected && userInput.value !== username) {
                                forceFillReactInput(userInput, username);
                            }
                            if (passInput.value !== password) {
                                forceFillReactInput(passInput, password);
                            }
                            const submitBtn = findGateSubmitButton() || findLoginSubmitButton(passInput);
                            if (submitBtn) {
                                if (isBtnBusyOrDisabled(submitBtn)) return;
                                window.__lastLoginSubmitTime = Date.now();
                                realClick(submitBtn);
                                if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                                    window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog(tabIdx, "Đã ấn [Xác Định] đăng nhập: " + username);
                                }
                                return;
                            }
                            window.__lastLoginSubmitTime = Date.now();
                            try {
                                passInput.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, which: 13 }));
                                passInput.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, which: 13 }));
                            } catch(e) {}
                            if (passInput.form) {
                                try {
                                    if (typeof passInput.form.requestSubmit === 'function') {
                                        passInput.form.requestSubmit();
                                    } else {
                                        passInput.form.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
                                    }
                                } catch(e) {}
                            }
                        } catch(e) {}
                    }

                    setTimeout(confirmAndClickSubmit, 320);

                    return "FILLED_AND_CLICKED_XAC_DINH";
                }

                const playHereBtn = findBestButtonByText(function(txtNA, rawTxt) {
                    if (rawTxt === 'Chơi ở đây' || rawTxt === 'Chơi ở tab này' ||
                        rawTxt.includes('Chơi ở đây') || rawTxt.includes('Chơi ở tab này') ||
                        rawTxt.includes('Tiếp tục ở đây') || rawTxt.includes('Kết nối lại')) {
                        return 100;
                    }
                    return -1;
                });
                if (playHereBtn && !isBtnBusyOrDisabled(playHereBtn)) {
                    if (Date.now() - (window.__lastDialogActionTime || 0) < 2500) {
                        return "WAITING_LOGIN_RESPONSE";
                    }
                    window.__lastDialogActionTime = Date.now();
                    realClick(playHereBtn);
                    if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                        window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog(tabIdx, "⚡ Đã tự động chọn [Chơi ở đây] để tiếp tục chạy game");
                    }
                    return "CLICKED_PLAY_HERE";
                }

                const agreeBtn = findBestButtonByText(function(txtNA) {
                    if (txtNA === 'toi dong tinh' || txtNA === 'dong tinh' || txtNA === 'toi da hieu') {
                        return 100;
                    }
                    return -1;
                });
                if (agreeBtn && !isBtnBusyOrDisabled(agreeBtn)) {
                    if (Date.now() - (window.__lastDialogActionTime || 0) < 2500) {
                        return "WAITING_LOGIN_RESPONSE";
                    }
                    window.__lastDialogActionTime = Date.now();
                    realClick(agreeBtn);
                    if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                        window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog(tabIdx, "Đã nhấn [Tôi Đồng Tình]");
                    }
                    return "CLICKED_AGREE";
                }

                const loginModalResult = tryFillAndSubmitLoginModal();
                if (loginModalResult !== null) {
                    return loginModalResult;
                }

                const charSelectResult = tryEnterCharacterSelect();
                if (charSelectResult !== null) {
                    return charSelectResult;
                }

                const charSelectConfirmBtn = findBestButtonByText(function(txtNA, rawTxt, el) {
                    if (txtNA === 'xac dinh' || txtNA === 'vao game' || txtNA === 'bat dau') {
                        const isLoginModal = el.closest('form') || (el.parentElement && el.parentElement.querySelector && el.parentElement.querySelector('input[type="password"]'));
                        if (!isLoginModal) return 130;
                    }
                    return -1;
                });

                if (charSelectConfirmBtn && !isBtnBusyOrDisabled(charSelectConfirmBtn) && getVisibleEditableInputs().length === 0) {
                    if (Date.now() - (window.__lastCharGoTime || 0) < 4500) {
                        return 'WAITING_CHAR_SELECT';
                    }
                    window.__lastCharGoTime = Date.now();
                    const sects = ['Thiếu Lâm', 'Thiên Vương', 'Đường Môn', 'Ngũ Độc', 'Nga Mi', 'Thúy Yên', 'Cái Bang', 'Thiên Nhẫn', 'Võ Đang', 'Côn Lôn', 'Đoàn Thị'];
                    let charCard = null;
                    for (const el of allClickables) {
                        if (!isVisible(el)) continue;
                        const txt = (el.innerText || '');
                        if (txt.includes('Cấp') || txt.includes('Lv') || sects.some(s => txt.includes(s))) {
                            const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
                            if (rect.height > 20 && rect.width > 50) {
                                charCard = el;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    if (charCard) {
                        realClick(charCard);
                    }

                    setTimeout(function() {
                        try {
                            if (charSelectConfirmBtn.isConnected && !isBtnBusyOrDisabled(charSelectConfirmBtn)) {
                                realClick(charSelectConfirmBtn);
                            }
                        } catch(e) {}
                        if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                            window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog(tabIdx, "Đã chọn nhân vật và ấn vào game");
                        }
                    }, 350);

                    return "CLICKED_CHARACTER_SELECT";
                }

                const startGameBtn = findBestButtonByText(function(txtNA) {
                    if (txtNA === 'bat dau tro choi') return 120;
                    if (txtNA.includes('bat dau tro choi')) return 90;
                    return -1;
                });

                if (startGameBtn && !isBtnBusyOrDisabled(startGameBtn)) {
                    if (Date.now() - (window.__lastStartBtnTime || 0) < 3000) {
                        return "WAITING_LOGIN_RESPONSE";
                    }
                    window.__lastStartBtnTime = Date.now();
                    realClick(startGameBtn);
                    if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                        window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog(tabIdx, "Đã nhấn [Bắt Đầu Trò Chơi] -> Mở khung Đăng Nhập");
                    }
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        try { tryFillAndSubmitLoginModal(); } catch(e) {}
                    }, 750);
                    return "CLICKED_BAT_DAU_TRO_CHOI";
                }

                const otherOpenBtn = findBestButtonByText(function(txtNA) {
                    if (txtNA === 'vao game' || txtNA === 'vao giang ho' || txtNA === 'bat dau' ||
                        txtNA === 'vao tro choi' || txtNA === 'choi ngay') {
                        return 80;
                    }
                    return -1;
                });
                if (otherOpenBtn && !isBtnBusyOrDisabled(otherOpenBtn)) {
                    if (Date.now() - (window.__lastStartBtnTime || 0) < 3000) {
                        return "WAITING_LOGIN_RESPONSE";
                    }
                    window.__lastStartBtnTime = Date.now();
                    realClick(otherOpenBtn);
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        try { tryFillAndSubmitLoginModal(); } catch(e) {}
                    }, 750);
                    return "CLICKED_OPEN_LOGIN";
                }

                if (inGameNow()) {
                    if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onInGameDetected) {
                        window.AndroidBridge.onInGameDetected(tabIdx);
                    }
                    return "IN_GAME";
                }

                if (!byId('modeSw') && !byId('stageLbl') && !byId('csel') && !authGateRoot()) {
                    const bodyText = document.body ? (document.body.innerText || '') : '';
                    const hasBattleMenu = (bodyText.includes('Hành trang') && (bodyText.includes('Kỹ năng') || bodyText.includes('Võ công') || bodyText.includes('Hệ thống'))) ||
                                         bodyText.includes('Chế độ :') ||
                                         bodyText.includes('Chế độ:') ||
                                         bodyText.includes('Chế Độ :') ||
                                         bodyText.includes('Chế Độ:');
                    if (hasBattleMenu) {
                        if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onInGameDetected) {
                            window.AndroidBridge.onInGameDetected(tabIdx);
                        }
                        return "IN_GAME";
                    }
                }

                return "WAITING_SCREEN";
            } catch (err) {
                if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                    window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog($tabIndex, "Lỗi script: " + err.message);
                }
                return "ERROR: " + err.message;
            }
        })($safeUserJson, $safePassJson);
        """.trimIndent()
    }

    fun buildPeriodicWatchdogAndScanScript(tabIndex: Int): String {
        return """
        (function() {
            try {
                const tabIdx = $tabIndex;
                
                const activeEl = document.activeElement;
                if (activeEl) {
                    const tag = (activeEl.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
                    if (tag === 'SELECT' || tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA') {
                        return "USER_IS_INTERACTING";
                    }
                }
                
                if (window.__isUserTouching) {
                    return "USER_IS_TOUCHING_FROM_KOTLIN";
                }

                function norm(str) {
                    return (str || '').toString().toLowerCase().normalize('NFC').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                }

                function noAccent(str) {
                    return norm(str)
                        .normalize('NFD')
                        .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
                        .replace(/đ/g, 'd');
                }

                function isVisible(el) {
                    if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
                    if ((el.type || '').toLowerCase() === 'hidden') return false;
                    const tag = (el.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
                    try {
                        if (el.getClientRects().length === 0 && tag !== 'BODY') {
                            return false;
                        }
                    } catch(e) {}
                    let cur = el;
                    while (cur && cur !== document.body && cur !== document.documentElement) {
                        if (cur.hidden === true) return false;
                        try {
                            const style = window.getComputedStyle(cur);
                            if (style && (style.display === 'none' || style.visibility === 'hidden' || style.visibility === 'collapse' || parseFloat(style.opacity) === 0)) {
                                return false;
                            }
                        } catch(e) {}
                        cur = cur.parentElement;
                    }
                    const isFormOrBtn = (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'BUTTON' || tag === 'SELECT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA' || el.getAttribute('role') === 'button');
                    try {
                        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
                        if (isFormOrBtn) {
                            if (rect.height > 0 || rect.width > 0 || el.offsetParent !== null) return true;
                            if (el.parentElement) {
                                const pRect = el.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
                                return pRect.height > 0 || pRect.width > 0;
                            }
                        }
                        return rect.width > 1 && rect.height > 1;
                    } catch(e) {
                        return el.offsetParent !== null;
                    }
                }

                function realClick(el) {
                    if (!el) return;
                    const target = el.closest('button, [role="button"], a, input[type="button"], input[type="submit"]') || el;
                    try {
                        const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
                        const cx = rect.left + Math.max(2, rect.width / 2);
                        const cy = rect.top + Math.max(2, rect.height / 2);
                        ['pointerdown', 'mousedown', 'pointerup', 'mouseup'].forEach(type => {
                            try {
                                target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(type, {
                                    view: window, bubbles: true, cancelable: true, clientX: cx, clientY: cy, buttons: 1
                                }));
                            } catch(e) {}
                        });
                    } catch(e) {}
                    if (typeof target.click === 'function') {
                        try { target.click(); } catch(e) {}
                    }
                }

                function cleanText(txt) {
                    if (!txt) return "";
                    return txt.replace(/\n+/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                }

                if (!window.__vlScanSt) {
                    window.__vlScanSt = { t: 0, st: null };
                    window.addEventListener('message', function (msgEvt) {
                        try {
                            if (msgEvt.source !== window) return;
                            const d = msgEvt.data;
                            if (!d || d.vlm !== 'st' || !d.st || typeof d.st !== 'object') return;
                            window.__vlScanSt.st = d.st;
                            window.__vlScanSt.t = Date.now();
                        } catch (errSt) {}
                    });
                }
                const stAge = window.__vlScanSt ? (Date.now() - (window.__vlScanSt.t || 0)) : 1e9;
                const stp = (window.__vlScanSt && stAge < 15000) ? window.__vlScanSt.st : null;
                const stInGame = !!(stp && stp.o === 'game');
                const stOutGame = !!(stp && stAge < 5000 && (stp.o === 'dangnhap' || stp.o === 'menu'));
                const modalEl = document.getElementById('modal');
                const isStealth = !!(document.documentElement && document.documentElement.classList && document.documentElement.classList.contains('__vlm-stealth'));
                const modalOpen = !isStealth && !!(modalEl && !modalEl.classList.contains('hidden'));

                // Tránh đọc document.body.innerText (gây layout reflow nặng) khi stp đã xác nhận in-game
                // và không có modal bật; chỉ đọc nội dung #modal hoặc khi chưa vào game.
                let bodyText = '';
                if (!stInGame) {
                    bodyText = document.body ? (document.body.innerText || '') : '';
                    for (const chatId of ['chatLines', 'u20lines', 'u20log', 'chatBox', 'logBox']) {
                        const cel = document.getElementById(chatId);
                        if (cel && cel.innerText) bodyText = bodyText.split(cel.innerText).join(' ');
                    }
                } else if (modalOpen) {
                    bodyText = modalEl.innerText || modalEl.textContent || '';
                }
                const bodyNA = bodyText ? noAccent(bodyText) : '';
                const allElements = Array.from(
                    document.querySelectorAll(
                        (!stInGame || modalOpen)
                            ? 'button, [role="button"], a, div, span, p, label'
                            : 'button, [role="button"], a'
                    )
                );

                for (const el of allElements) {
                    const txt = (el.textContent || el.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                    if (!txt || txt.length > 35) continue;
                    const isRetryBtn = (txt === 'Thử lại' || txt === 'Kết nối lại' || txt === 'Tải lại');
                    if (txt === 'Chơi ở đây' || txt === 'Chơi ở tab này' ||
                        txt.includes('Chơi ở đây') || txt.includes('Chơi ở tab này') ||
                        txt.includes('Tiếp tục ở đây') || txt.includes('Kết nối lại') ||
                        isRetryBtn) {
                        if (!isVisible(el)) continue;
                        if (isRetryBtn) {
                            const nowRt = Date.now();
                            let lastRt = 0;
                            try { lastRt = parseInt(sessionStorage.getItem('__vl_retry_at') || '0', 10) || 0; } catch(eRt) {}
                            if (nowRt - lastRt < 25000) continue;
                            try { sessionStorage.setItem('__vl_retry_at', String(nowRt)); } catch(eRt2) {}
                        }
                        realClick(el);
                        if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog) {
                            window.AndroidBridge.onGameLog(tabIdx, "⚡ Đã tự bấm [" + txt + "] tiếp tục phiên chơi");
                        }
                        return "CLICKED_PLAY_HERE";
                    }
                }

                if (bodyText && (bodyText.includes('Tài khoản đang mở ở nơi khác') || bodyText.includes('đang mở ở máy / tab khác') || bodyNA.includes('mat ket noi') || bodyNA.includes('ket noi bi ngat') || bodyNA.includes('phien het han'))) {
                    for (const el of allElements) {
                        const txt = (el.textContent || el.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                        if (!txt || txt.length > 35) continue;
                        if (txt.includes('Chơi ở đây') || txt.includes('Chơi ở tab') || txt.includes('Tiếp tục') || txt.includes('Kết nối lại') || txt === 'Thử lại' || txt === 'Đồng ý') {
